Motivation: 现有训练时序-语言对齐模型的方法存在自监督陷阱：LLM标注的质量受限于其自身的感知能力，数据永远无法教会模型超过标注者已有的知识。此外，大多数数据集使用单变量序列，但真正重要的模式（跨通道相关性、超前滞后结构、共现异常）仅出现在多变量场景中，而这恰恰暴露了标注LLM的能力短板。这形成了一个三难困境：现有方法无法同时做到可靠、真实和可扩展。

Method: 作者提出"感知与描述解耦"的范式：由确定性代码从真实开源的多变量时序数据中计算一组统计量，LLM仅负责将这些预先计算好的事实进行语言化表达。LLM不擅长的感知交由计算完成，LLM专注于其擅长的表达。基于此构建了40亿参数的CGTime模型。

Result: CGTime在多变量理解任务上超越了规模大得多的通用模型。在其保留基准测试上，CGTime的多变量事实得分达到0.283，显著优于GPT-4o-mini的0.173和GPT-5.4-nano的0.203，且经Holm校正的配对显著性检验确认该优势对所有基线均成立。CGTime在生成描述中更准确地陈述了可验证的数值事实，并覆盖了更广泛的统计属性。

Conclusion: 通过将感知（计算）与描述（语言）解耦，CGTime成功突破了自监督陷阱和三难困境，以仅4B的参数量在多变量时序理解上实现了对更大通用模型的超越，证明了计算接地范式在时序-语言对齐任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Training multimodal models to align time series with language runs into a self-supervision trap. The usual recipe asks an LLM to read a series and write a description, so label quality is capped by the perceptual skill the model is supposed to learn. The data can never teach more than the labeler already knows. A second gap makes this worse: most datasets use a single variable, but the patterns that matter (cross-channel correlation, lead-lag structure, co-occurring anomalies) appear only with several variables, right where the labeling LLM's limits are most exposed. These two problems create a trilemma: existing methods are reliable, realistic, or scalable, but none achieves all three. We resolve this by decoupling perception from description. Deterministic code computes a set of statistics from real, open-source multivariate series; the LLM verbalizes those precomputed facts. Perception, which LLMs do poorly, is handled by computation, while the LLM handles expression. This produces CGTime, our 4B-parameter computation-grounded time-series-language model. CGTime outperforms far larger general-purpose models on multivariate understanding tasks: it attains the best multivariate fact score on our held-out benchmark (0.283 vs. 0.173 for GPT-4o-mini and 0.203 for GPT-5.4-nano), a gap that survives Holm-corrected paired significance tests against every baseline. It also states verifiable numerical facts in generated captions more accurately and covers a broader range of statistical properties.

</details>


### [174] [Disentangling 3D Modeling from Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05242)
*Haoze Sun,Jiequan Cui,Qingshan Xu,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DiSR框架，将3D感知与推理显式解耦：用现成感知模型重建结构化3D证据，再用LoRA微调的LLM进行符号推理，在空间推理基准上取得有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常通过大规模端到端训练联合学习隐式3D感知和推理，但现代感知模型擅长估计连续3D几何，LLM擅长组合性符号推理，两者的互补优势尚未被充分利用。因此，探索显式解耦感知与推理的替代范式具有研究价值。

Method: 提出Disentangled Spatial Reasoner (DiSR)框架：(1) 利用现成的专家感知模型将物理世界重建为结构化的3D证据；(2) 使用LoRA微调大语言模型，使其仅基于显式的几何证据执行推理。无需大规模3D VQA训练或复杂工具调用策略。

Result: DiSR在主流空间推理基准上取得了有竞争力的性能，同时具备更好的可解释性、模块化和计算效率。

Conclusion: 显式分离感知与推理是端到端建模的一种可扩展且有效的替代范式，DiSR框架验证了这一方向在空间智能领域的可行性和优势。

Abstract: In this work, we explore an alternative paradigm for spatial reasoning by explicitly disentangling 3D perception from reasoning, rather than jointly acquiring implicit 3D perception and reasoning through large-scale training. Our key observation is that modern perception models excel at estimating continuous 3D geometry, whereas large language models (LLMs) are particularly effective at compositional and symbolic reasoning. Motivated by these complementary strengths, we propose the Disentangled Spatial Reasoner (DiSR), a simple yet effective framework that reconstructs the physical world into structured 3D evidence using off-the-shelf expert perception models and fine-tunes an LLM with LoRA to perform reasoning solely over this explicit geometric evidence. Without large-scale 3D VQA training or complex tool-use policies, DiSR achieves competitive performance on popular spatial reasoning benchmarks. Beyond its strong performance, DiSR offers improved interpretability, modularity, and computational efficiency, demonstrating that explicit separation of perception and reasoning is a scalable and effective alternative paradigm to end-to-end modeling for spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [175] [Marginal Matching Does Not License Factorized Sampling: Auditing Conditional Style Leakage in Factorized Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05243)
*Duong Bach,Hai Nguyen Hong,Cuong Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 因子化生成模型通常通过匹配隐风格变量zs的边缘分布与固定高斯先验来正则化，并将其解释为风格与类别独立的证据。本文证明这种解释是错误的——仅匹配边缘分布无法约束类条件分布，导致风格隐变量仍可高度预测标签。作者通过精确分解揭示了该不匹配是因子化采样的四个必要条件之一，并通过实验证明消除它是必要但不充分的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因子化生成模型普遍声称通过将隐风格变量的边缘分布匹配到高斯先验，可以实现风格与类别信息的解耦（即独立）。但作者指出这一推理存在根本性缺陷：边缘分布匹配并不约束类条件分布，因此风格隐变量可能在类条件层面仍包含大量标签信息，导致声称的"解耦"有名无实。作者旨在揭示这一方法论缺陷，并提供理论和实证证据。

Method: 理论层面，作者推导了因子化采样所需的四个条件的精确分解，证明边缘分布匹配与类条件独立之间的差距是其中之一。实验层面，使用一个案例研究模型和四个代表性基线，在MNIST和CIFAR-10两个数据集上，通过六种独立扰动（模型容量、课程学习、先验几何、监督等）验证风格隐变量中的标签泄漏。同时评估了四种缓解策略（降低线性探针准确率至21%-46%）和两种后处理方法：后验条件先验（MNIST上外部评估类别生成达0.97，CIFAR-10仅0.41）与经验风格库（CIFAR-10达0.88）。

Result: 在全局MMD接近零的情况下，线性探针仍能以74%-100%的准确率恢复类别标签（随机水平10%）；模型聚类准确率达99.15%，但外部评估的类条件生成成功率仅16%。该标签泄漏在六种独立扰动下持续存在。四种缓解策略将探针准确率降至21%-46%，但类内依赖性基本未变。后验条件先验在MNIST上有效（0.97），但在CIFAR-10上效果有限（0.41），而经验风格库在CIFAR-10上可达0.88。

Conclusion: 仅基于风格隐变量边缘分布计算的任何散度度量都无法认证其与类别标签的独立性；仅报告边缘统计量不能验证因子化生成模型中通常声称的解耦性质。这对该领域普遍采用的评估方法提出了根本性质疑。

Abstract: Factorized generative models commonly regularize a latent style variable z_s by matching its marginal distribution to a fixed Gaussian prior and interpret this as evidence that the style representation is independent of class information. We show that this interpretation is incorrect. Matching only the marginal distribution places no constraint on the class-conditional distributions, allowing the latent style to remain highly predictive of the label despite appearing perfectly Gaussian in aggregate. We derive an exact decomposition showing that this mismatch is one of four conditions required for factorized sampling, and demonstrate that eliminating it is necessary but not sufficient to obtain the intended factorization.
  Empirically, our case-study model and four representative latent baselines achieve near-zero global MMD while still allowing a linear probe to recover class labels with 74%--100% accuracy (10% chance level). Our model reaches 99.15% clustering accuracy, whereas externally evaluated class-conditional generation succeeds only 16% of the time. This leakage remains under six independent perturbations involving model capacity, curriculum, prior geometry, and supervision across two datasets. Four mitigation strategies reduce probe accuracy to 21%--46%, although they leave within-class dependence largely unchanged. A post-hoc conditional prior improves externally evaluated class generation to 0.97 on MNIST without retraining but reaches only 0.41 on CIFAR-10, while an empirical style bank achieves 0.88 on CIFAR-10. These results demonstrate that no divergence computed solely on the marginal distribution of the style latent can certify independence from class labels, and that reporting marginal statistics alone does not verify the property commonly claimed in factorized generative models.

</details>


### [176] [PRISM: Priority-aware Rubric Internalization via Structured Multimodal Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05249)
*Xiaomin He,Dongling Xiao,Jiahao Xie,Ruiqi Lu,Qianle Wang,Zhongbin Guo,Wanxuan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRISM 针对真实多模态指令中多需求优先级不等的现状，提出"评分细则理解"（rubric comprehension）范式，将模型定位为按优先级逐条验证规则的执行者，并通过四阶段数据合成框架 PRISM 及配套评估集 PRISM-Eval，仅用10K合成样本即显著提升多模态大语言模型在多规则、优先级感知的指令遵循能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态训练数据大多将指令遵循简化为回答单一自包含问题，这与真实场景中多需求捆绑且重要性不等的多模态指令之间存在显著差距。现有评估依赖推理时裁判模型（judge model），缺乏确定性、可复现的评估手段。因此需要一种系统性的方法来训练和评估模型在多规则、优先级感知场景下的指令遵循能力。

Method: 提出 PRISM 四阶段数据合成框架：（1）生成 persona-task 对；（2）前缀引导的规则集生成；（3）质量过滤的评分细则；（4）结构化验证轨迹构建。将模型从"根据评分细则被评估的生成器"重新定位为"按细则逐条执行的验证者"。同时引入 PRISM-Eval 评估集，采用 Loose 和 Strict 两种指标，通过与固定标签的确定性匹配进行评估，无需推理时裁判模型。

Result: 仅使用10K合成样本，PRISM 将 Qwen3-VL-4B 在 PRISM-Eval 上的 Strict 准确率从 9.5% 提升至 30.1%，同时在通用基准上保持平均性能不下降。该增益可迁移至另外四个开源多模态大语言模型（覆盖 dense 和 MoE 架构）。

Conclusion: 结构化评分细则监督（structured rubric supervision）是通向多规则、优先级感知的多模态指令遵循的一条可扩展路径。PRISM 框架以极少的合成数据实现了显著的性能提升，且具有良好的模型架构泛化性。

Abstract: Real-world multimodal instructions often bundle multiple requirements with unequal importance, yet most multimodal training data still reduce instruction following to answering one self-contained question. We study this gap through \textbf{rubric comprehension}, which casts the model not as a generator measured against rubrics but as an \textbf{executor} that follows them: given an image and a typed, prioritized rubric, the model must verify each rule before producing an overall judgment. To support this setting, we propose \textbf{PRISM}, a four-stage data synthesis framework that produces persona--task pairs, prefix-guided rule sets, quality-filtered rubrics, and structured verification traces. We further introduce \textbf{PRISM-Eval}, whose Loose and Strict metrics use deterministic matching against fixed labels and therefore require no inference-time judge model. With only 10K synthesized samples, PRISM lifts Qwen3-VL-4B from 9.5\% to 30.1\% Strict accuracy on PRISM-Eval while preserving average performance on general benchmarks, and the gains transfer to four additional open-source MLLMs across dense and MoE architectures, suggesting that structured rubric supervision is a scalable path toward multi-rule, priority-aware multimodal instruction following.

</details>


### [177] [Beyond Full-Model Rollback: AuroSFT for Adapter-State Multi-Task Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05250)
*Yue Han,Ziniu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AuroSFT 提出了一种参数高效的多任务监督微调框架，将过拟合感知调度状态表示为紧凑可合并的适配器状态，冻结预训练主干网络，通过适配器训练、任务级峰值回滚和继续训练，在保持主干不变的情况下超越原版 msft。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务 SFT 将异构数据混合视为单一优化问题，但不同任务达到最佳泛化的时间点不同。msft 通过任务级推出、排除和回滚解决了这一不匹配，但其调度器状态以全模型检查点形式物化，导致阶段转换的存储、恢复和部署成本高昂。本文旨在用参数高效的方式重构这一状态。

Method: 提出 AuroSFT 框架：冻结预训练骨干网络，仅训练注入的适配器；在任务级峰值时回滚适配器检查点，并在剩余活跃任务混合上继续训练。在层级别，每个适配器对低秩权重因子（而非样本表征）应用 AuroRA 风格的自适应非线性层，使得更新对输入保持线性、秩有界且可精确合并到冻结投影中。

Result: 在保留骨干网络的比较协议下，AuroSFT 达到 61.36% 的平均准确率，对比对应 msft 参考行的 59.85%，并在全部五个骨干网络上均获得更高准确率。

Conclusion: AuroSFT 成功将过拟合感知多任务 SFT 的调度状态转化为参数高效的适配器形式，在降低存储和部署成本的同时实现了更优的泛化性能。

Abstract: Multi-task supervised fine-tuning (SFT) often casts a heterogeneous data mixture as a single optimization problem, even though different tasks may reach their best generalization at different times. msft exposes this mismatch through task-wise roll-out, exclusion, and rollback, but its original formulation materializes the scheduler state as full-model checkpoints, making stage transitions costly to store, restore, and deploy. This paper introduces AuroSFT, a parameter-efficient framework that recasts the carried state of overfitting-aware multi-task SFT as a compact, mergeable adapter state. AuroSFT freezes the pretrained backbone, trains only injected adapters, rolls back adapter checkpoints at task-wise peaks, and continues on the remaining active mixture. At the layer level, each adapter applies an AuroRA-inspired adaptive nonlinear layer to a low-rank weight factor rather than to the sample representation. The resulting update remains linear in the input, rank-bounded, and exactly mergeable into the frozen projection. Under the retained-backbone comparison protocol, AuroSFT achieves 61.36% average accuracy, compared with 59.85% for the corresponding msft reference row, and obtains higher accuracy on all five backbones. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroSFT-80D1.

</details>


### [178] [Beyond Rotations: AuroOFT for Expressive Quantized Orthogonal Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05253)
*Yue Han,Dianlin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AuroOFT在量化正交微调(QOFT)基础上引入零初始化门控低秩非线性残差分支，在不破坏量化兼容性的前提下实现输入自适应的非线性修正，在Qwen2.5模型上显著优于QOFT和QLoRA，同时节省32-45%可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QOFT方法中，任务特定更新被限制为线性正交变换，缺乏输入依赖的非线性修正能力，这制约了低比特量化语言模型在参数高效微调中的表达能力。

Method: 提出AuroOFT：保留QOFT作为稳定的量化兼容分支，同时为每个适配的线性层附加一个零起始门控低秩非线性残差分支。具体包括：(1) 将激活映射到RMS归一化的紧凑潜在空间；(2) 采用自适应非线性基函数，配合有界或token依赖的门控机制；(3) 零初始化的上投影矩阵确保初始化时与QOFT功能等价，正交性仅作为分支级稳定性属性而非组合非线性层的属性。

Result: 在Qwen2.5 1.5B/3B设置下，AuroOFT相比匹配的QOFT在Macro-6指标上提升1.30-2.70%，超过QLoRA达6.52-10.62%，且相比QLoRA节省32.3-44.7%的可训练参数。

Conclusion: AuroOFT成功将非线性自适应能力引入量化正交微调框架，在保持量化兼容性和参数效率的同时，显著提升了模型在下游任务上的性能，验证了分支级正交稳定性策略的有效性。

Abstract: Quantized orthogonal fine-tuning (qoft) enables parameter-efficient adaptation of low-bit language models by learning structured activation rotations before frozen quantized weights. However, its task-specific updates remain constrained to linear orthogonal transformations, limiting input-dependent nonlinear corrections. We introduce AuroOFT, which keeps qoft as a stable quantization-compatible branch while attaching a zero-start gated low-rank nonlinear residual to each adapted linear layer. AuroOFT maps activations into an RMS-normalized compact latent space and uses adaptive nonlinear bases with bounded or token-dependent gating. The zero-initialized up projection makes AuroOFT functionally identical to qoft at initialization, while orthogonality remains a branch-level stability property rather than a property of the combined nonlinear layer. Under matched data, optimization, decoding, and parser protocols, AuroOFT improves Macro-6 over matched qoft by 1.30-2.70% on the 1.5B/3B Qwen2.5 settings, exceeds QLoRA by 6.52-10.62%, and saves 32.3-44.7% trainable parameters relative to QLoRA in representative scales. The small exam-style multiple-choice math set is treated only as a protocol-sensitivity diagnostic. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroOFT-F3FD.

</details>


### [179] [FlowAdam: Implicit Regularization via Geometry-Aware Soft Momentum Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06652)
*Devender Singh,Tarun Sheel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowAdam 是一种混合优化器，在 Adam 基础上引入 ODE 梯度流积分。核心创新是软动量注入（Soft Momentum Injection），在 EMA 统计量检测到病态曲率时平滑切换至 ODE 模式，避免朴素混合方案的训练崩溃。在耦合优化任务上，低秩矩阵/张量恢复的 held-out 误差降低 10-22%，Jester 数据集降低 6%，同时保持标准任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam 的对角预处理矩阵是坐标系依赖的，难以处理参数间的稠密或旋转耦合（如矩阵分解、张量分解、图神经网络），因为它将每个参数独立对待。亟需一种能处理此类耦合结构又不牺牲 Adam 在标准任务上优势的优化器。

Method: 提出 FlowAdam：以 Adam 为基础，当 EMA 统计量检测到损失地形困难时，切换至经过裁剪的 ODE 梯度流积分。核心贡献是"软动量注入"机制，在模式切换时对 ODE 速度和 Adam 动量进行混合，避免直接替换导致的训练崩溃。

Result: 在耦合优化基准上，低秩矩阵/张量恢复的 held-out 误差降低 10-22%，Jester 真实协同过滤数据集降低 6%，超越调优的 Lion 和 AdaBelief；在 CIFAR-10 等良态任务上与 Adam 持平。MovieLens-100K 实验证实收益源自耦合参数交互而非偏差估计。消融实验表明，硬替换会使准确率从 100% 降至 82.5%。

Conclusion: FlowAdam 通过 ODE 梯度流积分有效处理参数耦合问题，软动量注入是实现稳定切换的关键。该优化器在耦合密集型任务上显著优于 Adam 及其变体，同时在标准任务上保持竞争力，证明混合优化策略的实用价值。

Abstract: Adaptive moment methods such as Adam use a diagonal, coordinate-wise preconditioner based on exponential moving averages of squared gradients. This diagonal scaling is coordinate-system dependent and can struggle with dense or rotated parameter couplings, including those in matrix factorization, tensor decomposition, and graph neural networks, because it treats each parameter independently. We introduce FlowAdam, a hybrid optimizer that augments Adam with continuous gradient-flow integration via an ordinary differential equation (ODE). When EMA-based statistics detect landscape difficulty, FlowAdam switches to clipped ODE integration. Our central contribution is Soft Momentum Injection, which blends ODE velocity with Adam's momentum during mode transitions. This prevents the training collapse observed with naive hybrid approaches. Across coupled optimization benchmarks, the ODE integration provides implicit regularization, reducing held-out error by 10-22% on low-rank matrix/tensor recovery and 6% on Jester (real-world collaborative filtering), also surpassing tuned Lion and AdaBelief, while matching Adam on well-conditioned workloads (CIFAR-10). MovieLens-100K confirms benefits arise specifically from coupled parameter interactions rather than bias estimation. Ablation studies show that soft injection is essential, as hard replacement reduces accuracy from 100% to 82.5%.

</details>


### [180] [An Emerging Retail Portfolio Management Application: Personalized, Tax-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Natural Language Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05255)
*Ramin Pishehvar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个面向散户投资者的个性化、税务感知投资组合管理系统，通过FastAPI后端与Web仪表盘，利用三阶段强化学习系统（跨资产编码器、MoE分配策略、LoRA适配器）将用户的自然语言投资目标转化为实盘集成（Alpaca API）的投资组合推荐，目前已完成集成测试但尚未对真实用户开放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散户投资者无法获得机构客户享有的个性化、税务感知的投资组合管理服务——现有智能投顾采用静态规则分配，机构级系统则有账户最低门槛和技术栈壁垒。该工作旨在弥合这一差距，为个人投资者提供低门槛、可落地的智能投资管理工具。

Method: 系统架构包含：（1）FastAPI后端与Web仪表盘，支持自然语言描述投资目标并路由至六种投资指令之一；（2）三阶段强化学习系统——①自监督跨资产编码器（学习资产间表示），②混合专家（MoE）分配策略配合学习型意图路由器进行资产配置，③轻量级LoRA适配器基于用户已披露的经纪行为实现个性化推荐，无需重训共享模型；（3）完整的工程基础设施：多用户认证、预览后确认的信任优先流程、每日邮件摘要、可审计的动作完整性链；（4）单元测试和端到端集成测试覆盖，并与Alpaca纸交易API对接；（5）14天前向回测与Bootstrap置信区间作为初步验证。

Result: 系统功能完整，已完成端到端集成测试（对接Alpaca纸交易API），包含多用户认证、确认流程、邮件摘要和审计链。14天前向回测（含Bootstrap置信区间）提供了预部署阶段的初步验证。此外，报告了若干工程实践经验：隐性未激活的集成路径、第三方API调用挂起问题，以及端到端实证验证优于信任checkpoint元数据的价值。

Conclusion: 该系统作为一个新兴的、预部署阶段的应用程序，展示了一条通向真实用户部署的具体路径。文中坦率地声明尚未对真实用户开放，并以预部署验证而非生产级性能的标准来定位回测结果。所总结的工程经验教训对构建依赖外部实时数据源的应用RL系统具有泛化参考价值。

Abstract: Retail investors lack access to the kind of personalized, tax-aware portfolio management that institutional clients take for granted -- existing robo-advisors use static, rule-based allocation, and institutional-grade systems require account minimums and technology stacks unavailable to individual investors. We present a fully built, integration-tested application that closes this gap: a FastAPI backend and web dashboard that let a user describe an investment goal in plain language (e.g. "I want steady growth but need to sell some shares next month for a down payment"), routes that goal to one of six investment mandates, and produces a live, broker-integrated portfolio recommendation from athree-phase reinforcement learning system -- a self-supervised cross-asset encoder, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) allocation policy with a learned intent router, and a lightweight LoRA adapter that personalizes recommendations from an individual's revealed brokerage behavior without retraining the shared model. The system is functionally complete and integration-tested end-to-end against a live brokerage API (Alpaca, paper-trading mode), including multi-user authentication, a trust first preview-before-apply confirmation flow, daily email digests, and an auditable action-integrity chain, but has not yet been opened to real end-users; we report this honestly as an emerging, pre-deployment application with a concrete path to full deployment, alongside 14-day walk-forward backtests (bootstrapped confidence intervals included) as preliminary, pre-deployment validation rather than production performance. We also report several practical engineering lessons -- silently-inactive integration paths, hanging third-party API calls, and the value of end-to-end empirical verification over trusting checkpoint metadata -- that we believe generalize to other applied RL systems built on external, live data sources.

</details>


### [181] [The Loss Does Not See the Basis, but Adam Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05136)
*Devender Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了优化器的规范等变性（gauge equivariance）是梯度流低秩偏好能否被继承的关键条件。梯度下降、动量法和共享标量Adam满足该条件，而坐标自适应方法（如标准Adam、RMSProp）则破坏了这种对称性，导致低秩偏差丧失。作者通过矩阵感知、Transformer注意力分析和高光谱实验，系统证明了优化器的基底选择深刻影响其收敛到的插值解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度下降在分解模型上隐式偏向低秩解，但Adam从相同的小初始化出发却不具备该偏好。现有理论无法解释为何不同优化器在相同损失景观下收敛到结构性质迥异的解，亟需揭示优化器对称性与隐式正则化之间的机理联系。

Method: 作者利用矩阵分解参数化的规范对称性——损失在 (U,V) → (UQ, VQ) 下不变——建立分析框架。首先给出规范等变优化器的必要条件，导出结构定理：无记忆等变更新规则恰好是Gram确定左预条件子。进而证明传递定理，将梯度流的路径性质迁移到共享标量流。在欠定矩阵感知任务上对九种更新规则进行排序，引入从坐标自适应到共享标量预条件的一参数族，隔离各向异性效应。通过"谱调度"实验调和关于Muon的矛盾结论，并在Transformer中测量不同优化器下 per-head 不变量 W_QᵀW_K 的差距。最后在两个高光谱数据集上，匹配训练损失下对比梯度下降与Adam的泛化能力及有效秩。

Result: （1）规范等变性是低秩恢复的必要非充分条件；梯度下降、动量、共享标量Adam、Muon、Shampoo满足，Adam、RMSProp等不满足。（2）一参数族实验确认各向异性是破坏低秩偏好的原因，且单调恢复。（3）Muon的谱调度调和了已有矛盾：等速率更新可精确恢复低秩目标，但谱尾增大时优势消失。（4）Transformer中，Adam第一步即分离两个规范等效初始化，最终 per-head 不变量 W_QᵀW_K 的Frobenius距离相差56%，而等变优化器保持在浮点精度。（5）高光谱数据上，梯度下降在最低采样密度下将测试误差降低43-44%，且有效秩更低。

Conclusion: 优化器的选择不仅仅是调参细节，而是决定模型在过参数化空间中收敛到何种插值解的关键决策。规范等变性是继承梯度流隐式低秩偏差的枢纽，坐标自适应优化器因各向异性破坏了该对称性。这一发现对大规模模型训练中优化器选型具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Gradient descent on a factored model $W = UV^\top$ is implicitly biased toward low-rank solutions, while Adam, starting from the same small initialization, is not. We trace the difference to the gauge symmetry of the loss, its invariance under $(U, V) \mapsto (UQ, VQ)$. Gradient flow's low-rank mechanism is available to an optimizer only if that optimizer is gauge-equivariant, a condition necessary for the transfer but not sufficient for low-rank recovery. Gradient descent, momentum, "shared-scalar" Adam, Muon, and Shampoo satisfy it. Adam, RMSProp, and the other coordinate-wise methods do not. A structure theorem characterizes the memoryless equivariant rules as exactly the Gram-determined left preconditioners, and a transfer theorem carries gradient flow's pathwise properties to common-scalar flows. We then sort nine update rules on underdetermined matrix sensing by recovery error against the planted ground truth. A one-parameter family from coordinate-wise to shared-scalar preconditioning restores the bias monotonically, isolating anisotropy as the cause. A "spectral schedule" reconciles two opposing reports about Muon: equal-rate updates recover exactly low-rank targets but lose their edge as the spectral tail grows. In transformers, Adam separates two gauge-equivalent initializations at the first step, where the equivariant optimizers stay at float precision, and ends with the per-head invariants $W_Q^\top W_K$ 56% apart in relative Frobenius distance, a gap no per-head rotation can close. On two hyperspectral datasets at matched training loss, gradient descent cuts held-out error by 43-44% at the lowest sampling density, and at lower effective rank. Basis choice is therefore not a tuning detail but a decision about which interpolant the optimizer selects.

</details>


### [182] [Evaluating Machine Learning Models for Post-Wildfire Debris-Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05265)
*Quinn Ledingham,Zhengsen Xu,Yimin Zhu,Zack Dewis,Mabel Heffring,Saeid Taleghanidoozdoozan,Motasem Alkayid,Megan Greenwood,Lincoln Linlin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了15种机器学习模型用于野火后泥石流预测，发现TabPFN模型在未增强数据下表现最佳（威胁评分0.637），SHAP分析揭示短历时降雨强度和累积降雨量是最关键特征，而基于TabPFN生成的合成数据增强可进一步提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火后泥石流预测面临三大挑战：(1) 泥石流与非泥石流事件在特征空间中高度重叠，难以区分；(2) 模型需要可解释性以建立信任；(3) 训练数据有限，尤其泥石流正样本稀缺。现有研究缺乏对机器学习模型的系统性对比评估。

Method: 基于美国西部流域尺度的野火后泥石流观测数据，系统比较15种机器学习模型（含TabPFN）。采用重复分层交叉验证评估预测性能，使用SHAP值识别关键特征及其驱动方向。进一步利用TabPFN生成合成样本进行数据增强，评估其对各类模型性能的提升效果。

Result: (1) TabPFN在无数据增强条件下威胁评分最高（0.637），树模型紧随其后；(2) SHAP分析表明短历时降雨强度和累积降雨量一致排名最重要特征，火烧严重度和地形特征贡献相对较小；(3) 合成数据增强使除CNN外所有模型性能提升，深度学习模型平均威胁评分增幅最大（+0.041）。

Conclusion: 通过将严格的模型基准测试、可解释特征分析与合成数据增强相结合，该研究为改进野火后泥石流预测提供了一个全面框架，TabPFN及其生成能力在数据稀缺场景下展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Prediction of post-wildfire debris flows is critical for mitigating hazards to communities, infrastructure, and resources during intense rainfall in recently burned areas. However, identifying reliable machine learning models is complicated by overlapping debris-flow and non-debris-flow events in feature space, the need for model interpretability, and limited training data. This paper addresses these challenges through a systematic evaluation of machine learning models in terms of predictive performance, feature importance, and synthetic data augmentation. Using basin-scale observations of post-wildfire debris-flow events across the western United States, we compare 15 models, including the Tabular Prior-Data Fitted Network (TabPFN). Repeated stratified cross-validation shows that TabPFN achieves the highest unaugmented performance with a threat score of 0.637, closely followed by the best tree-based models. SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) are used to identify the features driving predictions, revealing that short-duration rainfall intensity and storm accumulation consistently rank highest, while burn severity and terrain features contribute less. We further evaluate synthetic data augmentation using TabPFN-generated samples to address the scarcity of debris-flow observations. Synthetic augmentation improves the performance of all models except CNN, with the largest mean threat score increase of +0.041 among the deep learning models. By combining rigorous model benchmarking, interpretable feature analysis, and synthetic data augmentation, this work provides a comprehensive framework for improving post-wildfire debris-flow prediction.

</details>


### [183] [Rectifying Geometric Misalignment: Online Source-Free Adaptation for Class-Imbalanced EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05315)
*Shiwen Chu,Shanglin Li,Motoaki Kawanabe,Reinmar Kobler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对在线脑机接口中类别分布动态变化（标签偏移）导致标准黎曼对齐失效的问题，提出OSPDIM——一种基于信息最大化的源无关在线无监督域适应框架，通过在切空间映射中引入流形约束的偏置参数，实时校正几何偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准黎曼中心化变换(RCT)等对齐方法隐含假设类别先验均衡，但在真实在线BCI场景中，标签分布动态变化（标签偏移）会导致目标数据分布在几何上产生错位。现有方法依赖全局批统计，无法处理在线流式数据的实时校正需求。

Method: 提出OSPDIM框架：(1)在切空间映射中引入受流形约束的偏置参数；(2)通过信息最大化(IM)优化该偏置参数，以纠正因数据流不平衡造成的几何偏斜；(3)实现在线、实时的几何偏差估计与校正，无需源数据或全局批统计。

Result: 在2D SPD矩阵上的仿真可视化了OSPDIM对标准中心化失效情况的校正效果；在多个运动想象数据集上的实验表明，OSPDIM在严重类别不平衡的在线适应场景中显著优于标准黎曼基线方法。

Conclusion: OSPDIM为实用化即插即用BCI系统提供了一种鲁棒的在线域适应方案，有效解决了标签偏移下黎曼流形对齐的几何失配问题。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) based Brain-Computer Interfaces (BCIs) often require unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) to generalize across subjects and sessions. While Riemannian alignment methods like the Riemannian Centering Transformation (RCT) are effective for handling covariate shifts, they implicitly assume balanced class priors. However, in realistic online BCI scenarios, the label distributions vary dynamically (label shift), causing standard alignment techniques to geometrically misalign the target data distributions. In this work, we propose OSPDIM (Online SPD manifold information maximization), a source-free online UDA framework designed to address label shifts on the Riemannian manifold. OSPDIM introduces a manifold-constrained bias parameter into the tangent space mapping, which is optimized via information maximization to correct the geometric skew caused by imbalanced data streams. Unlike offline methods relying on global batch statistics, OSPDIM estimates and corrects geometric bias on-the-fly. Simulations on 2D SPD matrices visually demonstrate that OSPDIM successfully rectifies the misalignment where standard centering fails. Extensive experiments on multiple motor imagery datasets show that OSPDIM significantly outperforms standard Riemannian baselines, particularly in challenging online adaptation scenarios with severe class imbalance, offering a robust solution for practical, plug-and-play BCI systems.

</details>


### [184] [QEvict: Recoverable Quantized KV Eviction for Attention-Drift-Robust Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05326)
*Ayushman Garg,Akshita Gupta,Shaswata Bhattacharya,Abhishek Gupta,Sandeep Kumar,Manoj Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QEvict 提出一种三级 KV 缓存管理方案，用可恢复驱逐替代传统的二元保留/删除策略，通过将中等重要性的 token 存入量化可恢复层，在固定内存预算下保留更广泛的历史上下文，显著改善了长上下文任务中的注意力遗漏和信息保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 KV 缓存驱逐策略基于注意力分数做二元决策，一旦 token 被驱逐便永久丢失。然而研究发现，随着解码过程中生成的 query 不断演化，token 和窗口的重要性会发生漂移——原本被判定为不重要而被驱逐的状态，在后续解码中可能会获得显著注意力。这种"不可逆"假设在解码阶段是脆弱的。

Method: 提出 QEvict，一种三级 KV 缓存管理方案：(1) 高置信度窗口保留全精度；(2) 中等置信度窗口存入量化可恢复层；(3) 低置信度窗口直接删除。解码过程中，累积注意力分数持续更新窗口重要性；当量化窗口重新变得重要时，将其反量化并提升至全精度层。同时引入 Future Missed Mass 和 Global LIR 两个诊断指标，分别衡量被丢弃状态在未来获得的注意力总量和历史不活跃区域的重新激活程度。

Result: 在长上下文理解、检索和推理基准测试上，QEvict 相比代表性的驱逐和量化基线方法均取得一致提升，有效减少了注意力遗漏，增强了信息保留能力。

Conclusion: KV 缓存驱逐不应是二元不可逆的。通过引入量化可恢复层，QEvict 在固定内存预算下实现了更灵活高效的缓存管理，证明了可恢复驱逐策略在长上下文推理中的优越性。

Abstract: Autoregressive large language model inference is increasingly constrained by the memory footprint of the Key-Value (KV) cache. A dominant line of work reduces this footprint by evicting tokens that appear unimportant under attention-derived scores. However, such policies make an implicit irreversible decision: once a token is evicted, it cannot become useful again. We show that this assumption is brittle during decoding. Token and window importance drift as generated queries evolve, causing standard eviction policies to permanently discard states that later receive substantial attention under the full-cache model. To characterize this behaviour, we introduce Future Missed Mass and Global LIR, two diagnostics that measure future attention assigned to discarded states and the reactivation of historically inactive regions. We propose QEvict, a three-tier KV-cache management scheme that replaces binary retain-or-delete eviction with recoverable eviction. QEvict maintains high-confidence windows in full precision, stores intermediate windows in a quantized recoverable tier, and deletes only the lowest-confidence windows. During decoding, cumulative attention scores update window importance and when a quantized window becomes important again, it is dequantized and promoted to the full-precision. Under a fixed memory budget, this design preserves broader historical context while retaining exact full precision for the most important regions. Across long-context understanding, retrieval, and reasoning benchmarks, QEvict consistently improves over representative eviction and quantization baselines, reducing missed attention and improving information retention

</details>


### [185] [Hybrid Probabilistic Zonotopes for Identifiable and Refinable Predictive Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05454)
*Zhen Zhang,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HProbZ 是一种新的神经网络概率输出头，将预测不确定性分解为离散模式选择、有界系统漂移和不可约随机噪声三种来源，通过 zonotope（ zonotope ）的二元、有界和随机生成元表示，并具有闭式似然。有界生成元跨步骤共享实现了代数耦合，支持单次前向传播即可更新所有剩余步骤的预测分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络概率预测头（如高斯混合或单一共形区域）无法分离实际预测任务中常见的三种不同不确定性来源：模式间的离散选择、选定模式内的有界系统漂移、以及不可约的随机噪声。需要一种能够同时建模并区分这三种来源的输出结构。

Method: 提出 Hybrid Probabilistic Zonotope (HProbZ) 输出头：将三种不确定性分别建模为 zonotope 的二元生成元（离散模式选择）、有界生成元（系统漂移）和随机生成元（随机噪声），通过卷积获得闭式似然。有界生成元跨预测步共享，实现未来预测间的代数耦合，使得观测一步即可在单次前向传播中精化所有剩余步的预测分布。证明了三种生成元在似然下可识别（至多排列），且 HProbZ 密度与任意有限高斯混合在表示上不同。

Result: 在代表性预测基准上的实验表明，HProbZ 相对于同编码器的混合基线具有有效性，同时提供了混合模型或凸共形预测器无法共同提供的结构性质，包括解析的逐模式风险和多模态无分布共形集。

Conclusion: HProbZ 成功将预测中的三种不同不确定性来源统一到单一框架中，不仅预测性能有效，还额外提供了可识别性、分布表示独特性、解析风险分解和多模态共形推理等结构优势。

Abstract: Probabilistic prediction heads in neural networks typically output either a Gaussian mixture or a single conformal region. Neither separates the distinct sources of uncertainty often present in real prediction tasks: a discrete choice among modes, bounded systematic drift within the chosen mode, and irreducible stochastic noise. We introduce the Hybrid Probabilistic Zonotope (HProbZ), an output head that represents these three sources as binary, bounded, and stochastic generators of a zonotope, and admits a closed-form likelihood by convolution. Sharing the bounded generator across prediction steps couples future predictions algebraically, so observing one step refines the predictive distribution at every remaining step in a single forward pass. We establish that the three generators are identifiable from the likelihood up to permutation, and that an HProbZ density is representationally distinct from any finite Gaussian mixture. The same shared structure provides analytic per-mode risk and distribution-free multi-modal conformal sets at inference time. Empirical analysis on representative prediction benchmarks supports the effectiveness of the design relative to same-encoder mixture baselines, while offering structural properties that mixture or convex-conformal predictors do not jointly provide.

</details>


### [186] [DG-FedReuse: Proxy-Gradient-Gated Cached-Update Reuse with Matched Sparse Uplink Accounting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05358)
*Rahil Aftab,Vineet Kumar Rakesh,Soumya Mazumdar,Tapas Samanta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DG-FedReuse 提出一种联邦学习通信优化机制，允许客户端在上行链路中复用年龄衰减的缓存更新，前提是随机头部梯度差异代理指标低于阈值。在六个图像分类数据集上实现了83.36-85.42%的上行数据节省，但精度略有下降。研究表明通信节省的结论高度依赖于计账边界(是否计入下行链路)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习反复进行本地优化和模型更新传输，通信开销巨大。现有Top-K稀疏化方法虽能压缩上行通信，但仍有进一步提升空间。作者旨在探索通过缓存旧更新并复用(reuse)来进一步减少上行通信量，同时控制精度损失。

Method: 提出DG-FedReuse机制：(1) 当随机头部梯度差异(stochastic head-gradient discrepancy)代理指标低于轮次相关阈值时，允许选中客户端提交经过年龄衰减(age-decayed)的缓存更新；(2) 设置硬性缓存年龄上限和最小新鲜客户端配额以约束复用行为；(3) 新鲜更新采用自适应逐张量Top-K数值域表示进行压缩；(4) 在仿真器中实现，使用50个虚拟客户端、Dirichlet标签异质性(α=0.5)、三种随机种子在六个图像分类数据集上评估，统一90轮通信预算。

Result: 在90轮预算下，DG-FedReuse实现83.36-85.42%的建模上行数据字段节省，对比匹配的Top-K FedAvg为76.88%；种子对齐的准确率差异为-5.29至-0.14个百分点。最佳测试准确率(测试控制检查点下)相对FedAvg差异为-2.38至+0.45个百分点。当计入对称密集模型下行链路后，整体节省降至41.68-42.71%，相对Top-K FedAvg的增量收益仅剩3.24-4.27个百分点，表明通信结论高度依赖计账边界。

Conclusion: DG-FedReuse在仿真器中展示了通过缓存复用进一步压缩上行通信的潜力，但精度有一定损失。作者明确声明该研究仅限于仿真器中的机制刻画，不声称具有无偏泛化性、端到端带宽缩减、运行时或能耗节省、更快的收敛速度，也不声称优于现有陈旧更新和惰性聚合方法。

Abstract: Federated learning repeatedly incurs local optimization and model-update transmission. We study DG-FedReuse, a simulator-level mechanism that allows selected clients to contribute age-decayed cached updates when a stochastic head-gradient discrepancy proxy remains below a round-dependent threshold. A hard cache-age limit and minimum fresh-client quota constrain reuse, while fresh updates use an adaptive per-tensor Top-K numerical-field representation. Experiments cover six image-classification datasets, 50 virtual clients, Dirichlet label heterogeneity (α=0.5), and three seeds. At a common 90-round budget, DG-FedReuse yields 83.36-85.42% modeled update-data-field uplink saving, compared with 76.88% for matched Top-K FedAvg; the seed-aligned accuracy differences range from -5.29 to -0.14 percentage points. Best-observed test accuracies obtained under test-controlled checkpointing are retained only as exploratory archival evidence and range from -2.38 to +0.45 percentage points relative to matched FedAvg. A symmetric dense-model-downlink sensitivity reduces the headline saving to 41.68-F42.71% and the incremental gain over Top-K FedAvg to 3.24-4.27 percentage points, demonstrating the dependence of communication conclusions on the accounting boundary. The study characterizes the proposed reuse rule in the implemented simulator; it does not establish unbiased generalization, end-to-end bandwidth reduction, runtime or energy savings, faster convergence, or superiority over existing stale-update and lazy-aggregation methods.

</details>


### [187] [Quantum-Structured World Models (QSWMs) for Predictive Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05371)
*Hailong Jiang,Emran Hossain,Feng Yu,Jianfeng Zhu,Guilin Zhang,Wulan Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文引入量子结构化世界模型（QSWM），利用复值表示和密度矩阵等量子启发数学结构构建世界模型，建立了经典包含性、预测充分性和结构化紧凑性三项基础性质，并在元胞自动机上进行了实验评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型多采用经典向量、概率分布或Transformer激活表示潜在状态，作者探索量子理论启发的数学结构（复值表示、密度矩阵潜在状态）能否为世界建模提供有用的归纳偏置。

Method: 提出QSWM框架，包含结构化潜在状态、潜在转移算子和测量启发的解码映射。建立三项基础性质（经典包含性、预测充分性、结构化紧凑性），并实例化复值和密度矩阵两种变体，在初等元胞自动机上进行评估。

Result: 复值QSWM在局部预测上展现出有潜力的性能，但在长时域 rollout 上表现受限，密度矩阵变体同样存在局限性。

Conclusion: 量子启发的结构化表示可为世界模型提供有意义的归纳偏置，尤其在局部预测方面，但长程建模仍面临挑战。

Abstract: World models learn latent states that summarize interaction histories, evolve over time, and support prediction, simulation, or planning. Most existing world models represent these states using classical vectors, probability distributions, recurrent hidden states, or transformer activations. In this paper, we introduce Quantum-Structured World Models (QSWMs), a quantum-inspired framework for predictive world modeling with structured latent states, latent transition operators, and measurement-inspired decoding maps. We study whether mathematical structures inspired by quantum theory, such as complex-valued representations and density-matrix-like latents, provide useful inductive biases for world modeling. We establish three foundational properties: classical inclusion, predictive sufficiency, and structured compactness. We then instantiate complex-valued and density-matrix-like QSWM variants and evaluate them on elementary cellular automata against strong classical baselines. Results show promising local predictive potential for complex-valued QSWMs, while also revealing limitations in long-horizon rollout, density-matrix variants

</details>


### [188] [Spectral Distillation: From Nonlinear Dynamics to Linear State-Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05416)
*Liane Galanti,Devan Shah,Shlomo Fortgang,Elad Hazan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过凸学习+可证明蒸馏的两阶段流水线，从非线性动力系统观测中提取紧凑线性状态空间（LDS）表示，无需直接求解非凸系统辨识问题，并给出了首个端到端的可证明方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非线性系统辨识需要求解非凸优化问题，困难且缺乏保证。研究者希望探索能否绕开非凸问题，通过线性状态空间表示来学习非线性动力系统，同时获得理论保证。

Method: 提出两阶段流水线：(1) 使用观测谱滤波（OSF）——一种凸方法，从观测数据中学习隐式谱预测器，与最优线性观测器竞争；(2) 通过谱到LDS蒸馏，将隐式预测器显式化为递归线性动力系统。核心理论给出蒸馏后LDS平均预测误差的分解：指数小的蒸馏误差项 + 由最优观测器Luenberger复杂度控制的OSF学习项。

Result: 主要定理证明了平均预测误差可分解为指数小的蒸馏项和OSF学习项，且该保证是维度无关的——仅依赖于观测器复杂度而非表示非线性系统所需的潜在维度。在线性LDS基准和MuJoCo行为克隆实验上，该流水线产生的紧凑LDS预测器匹配或优于直接训练的基线方法。

Conclusion: 首次给出了通过凸学习加可证明蒸馏从非线性动力学中提取最优事后LDS表示的端到端方法，为非线性系统线性表示学习提供了理论和实践上的新范式。

Abstract: Can nonlinear dynamical systems be learned through a compact linear state-space representation, without directly solving a non-convex system-identification problem? We give a provable pipeline for doing so. Starting from observations of an unknown nonlinear dynamical system, we first learn an implicit spectral predictor using Observation Spectral Filtering (OSF), a convex method that competes with the best linear observer for the system. We then apply spectral-to-LDS distillation to convert this predictor into an explicit recurrent linear dynamical system. Our main theorem shows that the average prediction error of the distilled LDS decomposes into an exponentially-small distillation term and the OSF learning term governed by the Luenberger complexity of the best observer. The guarantee is dimension-free: it depends on observer complexity rather than on the latent dimension needed to represent the nonlinear system. To our knowledge, this yields the first end-to-end provable method for extracting a best-in-hindsight LDS representation of nonlinear dynamics through convex learning followed by provable distillation. Experiments on linear LDS benchmarks and MuJoCo behavior cloning show that the train-then-distill pipeline produces compact LDS predictors that match or outperform directly trained baselines.

</details>


### [189] [The Tamed Subgradient Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm beyond Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06283)
*Iosif Lytras,Nikolaos Makras,Sotirios Sabanis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对具有非光滑、超线性梯度增长、非凸势函数的采样问题，作者提出亚梯度驯化非调整朗之万算法（SG-TULA），该算法直接在亚梯度上运行，无需光滑化处理。论文推导了Wasserstein-2距离下的非渐近收敛界，并给出了显式常数。在GPT-2架构的LLM正则化预训练势函数上验证了假设，SG-TULA的增强坐标变体在预训练中与精调的AdamW和Muon具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于朗之万扩散的采样方法在处理非光滑、超线性梯度增长和非凸势函数时面临困难：要么需要计算代价高昂的光滑化操作，要么缺乏非渐近理论保证。特别是在大语言模型预训练等大规模优化问题中，迫切需要一种既能直接使用亚梯度、又能应对超线性增长且具备理论保证的高效算法。

Method: 提出**亚梯度驯化非调整朗之万算法（SG-TULA）**：将朗之万扩散直接离散化在亚梯度上，避免光滑化操作；引入**驯化（taming）技术**处理超线性梯度增长，构造稳定的显式迭代格式。推导了Wasserstein-2距离下的非渐近收敛界，所有常数按维度和逆温度显式追踪。进一步给出关联优化问题的超额风险估计。在GPT-2架构的正则化预训练势函数上验证假设条件，并提出增强的坐标级变体进行实际预训练。

Result: （1）推导出亚梯度朗之万算法目前已知最优的非渐近Wasserstein-2收敛速率，常数按维度和逆温度显式表达；（2）在正则化LLM预训练势函数上（GPT-2系列）验证了算法假设以显式常数成立；（3）SG-TULA的增强坐标级变体在预训练中与精调后的AdamW和Muon竞争力相当，而后者目前无可比的非渐近理论保证。

Conclusion: SG-TULA为同时处理非光滑性、超线性梯度增长和非凸性的采样问题提供了首个拥有非渐近理论保证的实用算法。其在大语言模型预训练中的竞争力表明，理论上有保障的算法在实际大规模任务中也具备应用潜力。

Abstract: We study the problem of sampling from target distributions whose potentials are simultaneously non-smooth, subject to superlinear gradient growth, and non-convex. We introduce the Subgradient Tamed Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm (SG-TULA), a discretisation of the Langevin diffusion that operates directly on subgradients, without relying on computationally demanding smoothing procedures. To handle the superlinear regime, taming techniques are employed to produce a stable, explicit scheme. We derive non-asymptotic convergence bounds in Wasserstein-2 distance, with all constants tracked explicitly in terms of dimension and inverse temperature, improving upon the currently known rates for subgradient-based Langevin algorithms. We further provide excess risk estimates for the associated optimisation problem. We verify the assumptions, with explicit constants, for the regularized pretraining potential of a LLM in the GPT-2 lineage and the boosted coordinate-wise variant of SG-TULA pretrains the former competitively against finetuned AdamW and Muon, for which no comparable non-asymptotic guarantees are presently available.

</details>


### [190] [Perturbation Sensitivity at Convergence: A Simple Signal for Identifying Spuriously Correlated Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05419)
*Nilesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需组标注的伪相关样本检测方法：利用训练收敛后对输入施加固定扰动，观察预测翻转率来区分"遵循伪相关规则"与"被脆弱拟合"的样本，进而重平衡训练，大幅提升最差组准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有识别伪相关样本的方法依赖早期训练信号，需要选择干预轮次这一超参数，而该超参数通常需组标注验证数据来选定。作者试图消除对组标注和早停轮次的依赖。

Method: 核心思想是在模型充分收敛后（loss已无法区分两组），对输入施加固定扰动（如高斯噪声），比较扰动前后预测是否翻转。被"共享规则"分类的伪相关样本预测稳定，而被"逐样本特异配置"拟合的非伪相关样本预测脆弱、易翻转。通过两次前向传播即可标记样本，随后用检测到的样本重平衡训练。

Result: 在Waterbirds数据集上，使用检测样本重平衡训练后，最差组准确率从57.3%提升至80.8%，接近使用真实组标签的85.8%。

Conclusion: 训练收敛后通过输入扰动观察预测翻转是一种有效且简便的伪相关样本检测手段，完全无需组标注和早停轮次选择，在分布偏移场景下显著改善模型公平性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Models trained by empirical risk minimization on data containing spurious correlations achieve high average accuracy while failing on subpopulations where the correlation does not hold. Existing methods for identifying the affected samples without group annotations rely on signals from early training, which requires locating the epoch at which to intervene, a hyperparameter typically selected using group-labeled validation data. We show that a usable signal is available after convergence, when loss no longer distinguishes the two populations. Samples consistent with the spurious correlation are classified by a shared rule, while the remaining samples are fit through configurations specific to individual inputs and are correspondingly more fragile. Applying a fixed perturbation to a converged model's inputs flips the predictions of the latter far more often than the former. The resulting procedure requires two forward passes per training sample, no group annotations at any stage, and no early-stopping epoch. Using the detected samples to rebalance training raises worst-group accuracy on Waterbirds from 57.3% to 80.8%, against 85.8% with ground-truth group labels.

</details>


### [191] [Surv-IPTB: An Attention-Based Model for Estimating Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit with Survival Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06288)
*Lev V. Utkin,Stanislav K. Kogan,Andrei V. Konstantinov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Surv-IPTB，一种基于注意力机制的生存分析框架，用于估计个体治疗获益概率（IPTB）。该方法将 IPTB 估计重构为二分类问题，通过配对比较处理组与对照组患者，利用不精确概率处理右删失，并采用可学习的注意力机制聚合比较结果。实验表明，该方法在复杂非线性场景下显著优于 T-learner、S-learner 等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统个体治疗效应估计方法在生存分析中面临两大挑战：（1）右删失数据导致治疗效果不确定，难以精确量化；（2）复杂非线性协变量-结局关系下，现有的元学习器（meta-learner）方法性能显著下降。因此需要一种既能原则性处理删失、又能灵活建模非线性关系的IPTB估计框架。

Method: （1）将IPTB估计重构为二分类问题：对处理组与对照组患者进行跨组配对比较，判断治疗是否延长生存时间；（2）采用不精确概率表示处理右删失：当配对比较中至少一方删失时，以区间值概率刻画不确定性；（3）设计注意力机制：通过可学习的 query-key 变换实现数据驱动的配对比较加权聚合，同时为删失样本学习软类别概率；（4）模型以端到端方式训练，输出每位患者的个体化治疗获益概率。

Result: 在合成数据集（螺旋形、钟形、圆形特征空间）上的实验表明：（1）Surv-IPTB 在不同删失率和治疗效应强度下均保持鲁棒性能；（2）一致优于配备随机生存森林、Cox比例风险模型和Beran估计器的T-learner与S-learner基线；（3）尤其在复杂非线性场景中优势显著，传统方法出现明显退化时，Surv-IPTB仍能有效估计IPTB。

Conclusion: Surv-IPTB 将注意力机制与不精确概率相结合，为生存分析中的个性化治疗获益评估提供了一种可扩展且统计原则性强的解决方案，在复杂非线性条件下表现优异。代码已开源。

Abstract: This work presents a novel attention-based framework for estimating the Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit (IPTB) in survival analysis contexts. The proposed model, called Surv-IPTB, directly quantifies the probability that a specific patient will experience extended survival time under treatment versus control. We reformulate IPTB estimation as a binary classification problem, leveraging pairwise patient comparisons across treatment and control cohorts. The framework incorporates a principled handling of right-censored observations through imprecise probability representations, where uncertain treatment effects are characterized by interval-valued probabilities. An attention mechanism with learnable query-key transformations enables flexible, data-driven aggregation of pairwise comparisons, while simultaneously learning soft class probabilities for censored cases. Through extensive experiments on synthetic datasets with complex nonlinear structures, including spiral, bell-shaped, and circular feature spaces, we demonstrate that our approach maintains robust performance across varying censoring rates and treatment effect strengths. The model consistently outperforms meta-learner baselines (T-learner and S-learner) equipped with random survival forests, Cox proportional hazards, and Beran estimators, particularly in challenging nonlinear scenarios where conventional methods exhibit significant degradation. The results establish the proposed attention-based framework as a scalable and statistically principled solution for personalized treatment benefit assessment in survival settings. The code implementing the model is publicly available.

</details>


### [192] [IFlowNets: Extending Generative Samplers to Learn Strategies in Incomplete Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05422)
*Conor M. Artman,Nicholas Di,Scott Perkins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出信息流网络（IFlowNets），将生成流网络框架从完全信息博弈扩展至不完全信息博弈，修正了原有约束在此场景下的不可行性，并在三种标准博弈环境中验证了其与OSMCCFR及标准RL方法相当或更优的性能与速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注RL与CFR的混合方法以权衡计算速度与性能，但对不完全信息博弈中生成式采样框架的探索较少。AFlowNets虽在完全信息博弈中有效，但其约束在不完全信息场景下无法产生有效的策略密度和训练目标，亟需一个可推广的通用框架。

Method: 作者将Adversarial Flow Networks（AFlowNets）推广为Information Flow Networks（IFlowNets），使其适用于不完全信息博弈。理论上证明了AFlowNets原有约束在不完全信息条件下不可行，并提出了修正后的泛化约束，确保生成有效的玩家策略密度与训练目标，从而严格泛化了AFlowNets。

Result: 在三个标准博弈环境中的初步实验表明，IFlowNets在性能与速度上可与Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo CFR（OSMCCFR）及标准RL方法媲美甚至更优。

Conclusion: IFlowNets成功将生成流网络框架推广至不完全信息博弈，修正了原有约束的局限性，为博弈论中的生成式采样方法提供了更通用的解决方案，并在初步实验中展现了竞争力。

Abstract: While many algorithms blend reinforcement learning (RL) with counterfactual regret (CFR) methods to leverage tradeoffs in computational speed and performance, there are fewer investigations into generative sampling frameworks in game theoretic applications in incomplete information games. We extend a generative flow network framework, Adversarial Flow Networks (AFlowNets), to incomplete information games, called Information Flow Networks (IFNs). We prove that previously established constraints for generative flow networks in complete information games are inadmissible for obtaining valid densities (corresponding to player strategies) and a valid training objective. We show that our proposed generalization, IFlowNets, alleviates this issue and strictly generalizes AFlowNets. In preliminary results for three standard game environments, IFlowNets perform comparably to or better than Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret (OSMCCFR) and standard RL-based methods in performance and speed.

</details>


### [193] [Why the Third Axis Is Freedom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05423)
*Michael Timothy Bennett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证探索式建模（XM）的"第三预训练轴"本质是"自由度"（freedom），即模型行为所隐含的约束强度。作者通过理论证明和实验表明：XM 实际上优化的是自由度而非探索本身，自由度选择在分布偏移下优于 XM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前 XM 被提出时声称"探索"是继数据和参数规模之外的"第三预训练轴"，与生成表达性相关。作者质疑这一论断，指出真正的底层因素是"自由度"——行为约束的弱程度，且已有研究证明最弱约束的模型最可能泛化。

Method: 理论层面：证明 XM 平均损失取决于候选输出中至少一个落入可接受区域的概率，探索将"错过概率"提升至 K 次幂；对于 K>1，匹配概率随自由度单调递增。实验层面：(1) Forward XM 实验，增大 K 会提高或饱和所测自由度；(2) 训练 XM 候选池，使用无标签父上下文的自自由度选择器与验证集选择对比，29/30 案例中自由度胜出。

Result: 理论上，XM 损失与自由度的关系使 K>1 时高自由度模型更优。实验证实：增大 K 可提升自由度；自由度选择器在 29/30 案例中击败验证集选择。生成表达性仅是自由度的模式计数代理，丢失了对泛化至关重要的扩展结构信息。

Conclusion: XM 是手段，自由度才是目的。在分布偏移下，直接选择自由度比 XM 更有效。"第三轴"应被重新定义为自由度而非探索。

Abstract: In generative training, a model produces an output and is penalised for its difference from an example. With one output per comparison, a model that produces one common answer can outperform a model retaining a broader repertoire. Explorative Modeling (XM) produces $K$ outputs per comparison and updates on the closest, claiming exploration as a "third pretraining axis" associated with generative expressivity. Here I show the third axis is actually freedom, meaning the weakness of the constraint implied by a model's behaviour. Previous work showed freedom is a property of function rather than form. Parameters, architecture, minimum-description-length (MDL), and data can vary while the behavioural constraint remains unchanged. It was formally proved that weakest models are likeliest to generalise, and freedom selection beat MDL by 110-500\% in induction experiments. I prove average XM loss depends on the chance a candidate misses an acceptable region, with exploration raising miss probability to power $K$. For $K>1$, match probability rises with freedom. I then demonstrate empirically that XM optimises for freedom. In a Forward XM experiment, larger $K$ increased or saturated measured freedom, and increased freedom at every tested value under context-dependent targets. I trained XM candidate pools and compared validation selection with a freedom selector that read unlabelled parent contexts. Freedom won in 29 of 30 cases. Generative expressivity is a mode-count proxy for freedom, that discards the extension structure that gives freedom its generalisation significance. XM is a means, freedom an end, and selecting for freedom improved XM under distribution shift.

</details>


### [194] [EvoHarness-RL: Learning Self-Evolving Runtime Harness for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05446)
*Xuying Ning,Dongqi Fu,Tianxin Wei,Hanqing Zeng,Yuanchen Bei,Bingxuan Li,Zihao Li,Qifan Wang,Xiang Shen,Yifan Wu,Jiayi Liu,Hong Li,Yinglong Xia,Xiangjun Fan,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EvoHarness-RL框架，让LLM智能体学习离线构建"驾驭策略"（harness policy），在长时程任务执行中自主管理外部状态（信念、进度、经验），通过监督微调+成本感知GRPO实现96.9%成功率，并揭示"驾驭退火"和"驾驭进化"两种涌现动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程LLM智能体越来越依赖外部执行支持（状态维护、进度跟踪、工具调用、结果验证、经验复用），但现有方法通过提示、启发式或领域特定约定来手动设计外部工作空间及其使用策略，缺乏自动化学习机制。核心挑战在于：如何从噪声交互轨迹中形成状态，以及如何控制运行时对外部状态的访问。

Method: 提出EvoHarness-RL：将外部驾驭状态抽象为信念（Belief）、进度（Progress）和经验（Experience）三个策略可访问的维度（BPE）；首先通过监督式驾驭微调教会基座智能体驾驭动作空间及如何构建有用的外部状态；然后采用成本感知的GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）探索协调策略，选择性进行外部状态的读取、更新和整合。

Result: 在ALFWorld环境下基于Qwen3-8B模型，EvoHarness-RL达到96.9%成功率。揭示两种关键动态：(1)驾驭退火（harness annealing）——训练将反复出现的驾驭使用模式内化到模型策略中，使智能体从频繁调用驾驭转向选择性访问外部状态；(2)驾驭进化（harness evolution）——进度更新和经验整合将驾驭提炼为紧凑的、任务自适应的状态基底。

Conclusion: 长时程智能体受益于可训练的构建与协调外部驾驭工作空间的策略，而不仅仅是添加更强的工具或更大的记忆；EvoHarness-RL为自动学习外部状态管理策略提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents increasingly rely on external execution support to maintain state, track progress, invoke tools, verify outcomes, and reuse experience across interactions. However, effective harness use raises two coupled challenges: state formation from noisy interaction traces and runtime control over external-state access. Existing agents usually handle both through prompts, heuristics, or domain-specific conventions, leaving the external workspace and its usage policy manually engineered. To address this, we study the problem of harness policy learning, where agents learn harness policies offline and deploy them to construct and update external harness state online during runtime task execution. We introduce EvoHarness-RL, which exposes Belief, Progress, and Experience (BPE) as policy-facing harness state. Supervised harness fine-tuning teaches the base agent the harness action space and how to construct useful external state, while cost-aware GRPO explores coordination policies to selectively read, update, and consolidate that state during long-horizon interaction. Instantiated on ALFWorld with a Qwen3-8B LLM, EvoHarness-RL reaches 96.9% success and reveals two key dynamics: harness annealing, where training internalizes recurring harness-use patterns into the model policy and shifts the agent from frequent harness calls toward selective external-state access, and harness evolution, where progress updates and experience consolidation refine the harness into a compact, task-adaptive state substrate. These results suggest that long-horizon agents benefit from trainable policies for constructing and coordinating with external harness workspaces, beyond simply adding stronger tools or larger memories.

</details>


### [195] [Matrix Zonotopic Attention: A Context-Adaptive Value Projection for Set Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05472)
*Zhen Zhang,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了标准多头注意力中softmax路由（上下文依赖）与线性值投影（上下文无关）之间的不对称性，提出变换自由度（TDOF）度量目标算子的复杂度，并证明上下文刚性的注意力需要与TDOF成比例的深度，而上下文自适应的值函数族可在单层实现相同表达。基于此，作者提出矩阵Zonotopic注意力（MZAttn），以输入依赖的门控矩阵族替代固定值投影，在保持置换等变性的同时获得选择性架构优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准多头注意力存在架构不对称：query-key路由是上下文自适应的（通过softmax），但值投影是上下文无关的线性映射。这导致对置换不变集合目标表达能力受限，需要更深的网络来补偿。作者旨在通过形式化分析揭示这一限制，并设计更高效的架构。

Method: （1）定义变换自由度（TDOF）：衡量精确表示某个目标算子所需的输入依赖方向数，作为目标复杂度的理论度量。（2）深度分离分析：证明上下文刚性注意力需要深度Ω(TDOF)，而上下文自适应的值函数族只需单层。（3）提出MZAttn：将固定值投影替换为矩阵zonotope族——一个中心矩阵加上若干由输入依赖门控加权的生成矩阵之和，初始化时退化为标准多头注意力。

Result: 实验表明MZAttn的优势是选择性的：在高秩、稀疏组合依赖的集合预测任务上表现显著优于参数匹配的标准注意力；而在聚合统计类目标上，标准注意力已具竞争力，MZAttn提升有限，与TDOF理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 上下文刚性的值投影是标准注意力的表达瓶颈；TDOF为量化目标复杂度提供了有效工具；MZAttn通过上下文自适应的值映射在特定任务上实现深度效率的显著提升，且初始化兼容标准注意力，便于实际部署。

Abstract: Multi-head attention combines an input-dependent softmax routing with an input-independent linear value projection, so the per-sample operator mapping aggregated values to outputs is the same for every input set. We study the consequences of this asymmetry for permutation-invariant set targets. We introduce the Transformation Degrees of Freedom (TDOF) of a target operator, a complexity measure counting the input-dependent directions an exact representation requires, and present a depth-separation analysis showing that context-rigid attention needs depth proportional to the target's TDOF, whereas a single layer with a context-adaptive value family can represent the same target. Building on this analysis, we propose Matrix Zonotopic Attention (MZAttn), which replaces the fixed value projection with a context-adaptive matrix-zonotope family: a centre matrix plus a sum of generator matrices weighted by input-dependent gates. The construction reduces to standard multi-head attention at initialisation, preserves permutation equivariance, and admits a data-driven reachability interpretation. Experiments on a range of set-prediction tasks are consistent with the TDOF prediction that the architectural advantage is selective: it appears on targets that depend on the input set in a high-rank, sparsely combinatorial way, and is small on aggregate-statistic targets where parameter-matched standard attention is already competitive.

</details>


### [196] [KV-Skill: Forging Expertise in the Model's Native Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05475)
*Zhaowei Han,Xiang Zhang,Bing Han,Kai Liu,Danqi Hu,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KV-Skill 提出一种将任务知识存储为外部可分解算子的设计空间，冻结的LLM通过轻量接口读取。它支持两种路径：将文本技能注册为算子，或直接从任务奖励学习潜在算子。该方法在不增加prompt位置的条件下，在多个基准上一致优于文本提示和现有参数高效微调方法（LoRA、prefix tuning等），且支持多技能独立加载与共享。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前任务知识的存储方式存在根本性矛盾：文本形式（prompt）模块化但每次推理都需重新解释，效率低；权重更新（微调）高效但难以独立加载、移除或共享。作者希望设计一种兼具模块化与高效推理的知识表示方式。

Method: 提出KV-Skill设计空间，核心是外部可分解算子，通过轻量接口注入冻结骨干网络的键值（KV）注意力层。包含两条互补路径：(1) Registration——将文本技能通过文本派生算子转换，并训练共享的per-backbone接口；(2) Reward Learning——直接从任务奖励中学习紧凑的潜在算子（可选结合文本技能）。两条路径均不增加prompt的序列长度。此外通过秩分析验证文本派生算子的有效性依赖于任务对齐方向。

Result: 在10个基准、4个骨干网络（三个模型家族）上实验：(1) Qwen3.5-4B LiveMath上，Registration达77.2准确率，远超源文本技能(23.4)、SkillOpt(52.0)和SoftSkill(64.5)；(2) 匹配奖励训练和参数预算下，KV-Skill在8个设置中7个最优，优于soft prefixes、prefix tuning和LoRA；(3) 秩分析表明文本派生算子在每注入层仅需一个任务对齐方向即可保留几乎全部收益，而随机方向无效；(4) 单个共享接口可保留三个独立可加载的KV-Skill而无灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 任务知识可从文本或经验中获取，压缩为外部算子，并与骨干网络分离部署。KV-Skill实现了模块化与高效推理的统一，为任务知识的存储和迁移提供了新范式。

Abstract: Task knowledge is commonly stored either as text in the prompt or as an update to model weights. Text is modular but must be interpreted on every use, while weight adaptation makes the resulting capability difficult to load, remove, or share independently. We introduce KV-Skill, a design space of external factorized operators that a frozen language model reads through a lightweight interface. KV-Skill supports two complementary paths. Registration converts an authored text skill into a text-derived operator and trains a shared per-backbone interface. Reward learning develops a compact latent operator directly from task outcomes, with or without an authored skill. Neither path adds positions to the prompt. Across ten benchmarks and four backbones from three model families, converting text to a KV-Skill consistently makes the same procedural knowledge more effective. On Qwen3.5-4B LiveMath, registration reaches 77.2 accuracy, compared with 23.4 for the source text skill, 52.0 for SkillOpt, and 64.5 for SoftSkill. Under matched reward training and parameter budgets, KV-Skill gives the best result in seven of eight matched settings against soft prefixes, prefix tuning, and LoRA. A post-hoc rank analysis further shows that text-derived operators retain nearly all of their benefit with one task-aligned direction per injection layer, while matched random directions fail. Finally, one shared interface retains three independently loadable KV-Skills without measurable forgetting. These results show that task knowledge can be acquired from text or experience, compressed into an external operator, and deployed separately from the backbone. Code is available at: https://github.com/shawnzhg/KV-Skill

</details>


### [197] [Align-RAG: Alignment Is All You Need for TSFM In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05571)
*Mohammad Asadi,Soheil Hor,Bardiya Akhbari,Jack W. O'Sullivan,Tahoura Nedaee,Layne C. Price,Raviteja Anantha,Euan Ashley,Ehsan Adeli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Align-RAG 提出一种无需训练的检索增强预测方法，通过对检索到的历史-未来窗口进行闭式振幅重缩放和整数滞后相位对齐，使冻结的时间序列基础模型能动态利用检索上下文，在多个基准上超越需要训练适配器的现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前检索增强预测方法假设冻结的TSFM骨干网络无法自主融合检索上下文，因此依赖需要训练的自适应融合模块。本文质疑这一假设，旨在证明冻结骨干网络本身已具备动态利用检索样本的能力，且可通过简单的闭式对齐替代训练过程。

Method: 提出Align-RAG，一种无训练方法：对每个检索到的"过去-未来"窗口对，在输入冻结骨干网络之前，先进行闭式振幅重缩放（per-pair amplitude rescaling）和整数滞后相位偏移（integer-lag phase shift），使检索样本与当前查询在时序上对齐，从而让冻结模型直接利用对齐后的检索上下文进行预测。

Result: 在冻结的Chronos-Bolt上，Align-RAG在标准基准的7个数据集中均优于现有最优训练检索适配器（平均MSE降低3.75%）。在另外4种不同架构的冻结TSFM上，零样本MSE分别提升2.5%至13.7%，且无需针对各骨干调参。通过预测偏移分析，发现对齐后的demonstration诱导的预测偏移与闭式岭回归预测偏移一致，且未来shuffle对照实验排除了简单未来平均解释。

Conclusion: 冻结TSFM已天然支持检索样本的动态上下文利用，闭式对齐应作为检索增强预测中训练融合模块之前的默认基线方法。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented forecasting promises to adapt frozen Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) to new domains without fine-tuning, but recent methods typically rely on learned fusion modules, i.e., trained adapters that merge retrieved examples into the backbone's forecast, based on the assumption that frozen backbones cannot dynamically incorporate retrieved context on their own. We show this assumption is unnecessary. We introduce Align-RAG, a training-free method that applies a closed-form per-pair amplitude rescaling and integer-lag phase shift to retrieved past-future windows before they enter a frozen backbone's context. With no learned parameters, Align-RAG outperforms the state-of-the-art trained retrieval adapter on a frozen Chronos-Bolt on all seven datasets of the standard benchmark (avg -3.75% MSE), showing that the gains previously attributed to learned fusion are recoverable without any training. Align-RAG further improves zero-shot MSE on four additional frozen TSFMs with various architectures by 2.5% to 13.7% per backbone with no per-backbone tuning. To probe why alignment helps, we compare the frozen backbone's prediction shift under aligned demonstrations to the closed-form ridge prediction shift on the same pairs. We find that aligned demonstrations induce prediction shifts that track a closed-form ridge predictor on the same pairs, with a future-shuffle control ruling out a futures-averaging account. Together, these results indicate that frozen TSFMs already support dynamic in-context use of retrievals, and that closed-form alignment should be the default baseline for retrieval-augmented forecasting before any fusion module is trained. Code available at: https://github.com/masadi-99/align-rag

</details>


### [198] [LC-GRPO: Bridging Train-Inference Gap for Flow-Based GRPO with Langevin Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05600)
*Yingqing Guo,Hui Yuan,Zijian He,Mengdi Wang,Zheng Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LC-GRPO 提出一种带有 Langevin 校正的流式 GRPO 框架：每次 rollout 过渡先执行与推理对齐的 ODE 欧拉步，再施加一个以当前时间步边缘分布为目标的随机 Langevin 校正步。该校正所需的 score 直接从流速度中恢复，无需额外模型，且过渡保持各向同性高斯分布，具有可处理的似然。理论上证明在适当条件下，一步 Langevin 校正即可降低不完美 ODE 欧拉步的 Wasserstein 误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流模型推理时使用确定性 ODE 采样，但 GRPO 在线强化学习需要随机 rollouts 进行策略探索。现有方法用 SDE 替代 ODE 进行训练，但 SDE 的有限步离散化会因探索噪声增加而变得模糊，导致 RL 训练样本与测试时 ODE 采样器生成的样本之间存在不匹配。

Method: 提出 LC-GRPO：每次 rollout 过渡由两步组成——(1) 与推理对齐的 ODE 欧拉步，(2) 以当前时间步边缘分布为目标的随机 Langevin 校正步。Langevin 校正所需的 score 函数直接从流速度场中恢复（利用流模型速度与 score 的关系），无需额外训练 score 模型。所得过渡保持各向同性高斯形式，似然可解析计算，便于策略优化。理论上证明了该校正步可降低 Wasserstein 误差，且在相同随机性水平下比逆 SDE 的 Euler-Maruyama 离散化更精确。

Result: 在 SD3.5-Medium、FLUX.1-Dev 和 HunyuanVideo 上的实验表明，LC-GRPO 在文本到图像和文本到视频任务中持续改善奖励优化效果，保持生成质量，并显著缩小了随机训练 rollout 与确定性测试时 ODE 推理之间的差距。

Conclusion: LC-GRPO 通过 Langevin 校正机制有效解决了流模型中 GRPO 训练的分布不匹配问题，在多种模型和任务上验证了其有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Flow-based generative models are typically sampled by solving a deterministic ordinary differential equation (ODE), whereas online reinforcement learning requires stochastic rollouts for policy exploration and optimization. Existing GRPO methods for flow models therefore replace the inference-time ODE with a stochastic differential equation (SDE) during training. Although the ODE and SDE share the same marginal distributions in continuous time, their finite-step discretizations can differ substantially. In particular, SDE rollouts often become blurry as the exploration noise increases, creating a mismatch between the samples used for reinforcement learning and those generated by the test-time ODE sampler. We introduce LC-GRPO, a flow-based GRPO framework with Langevin correction. Each rollout transition first takes an inference-aligned ODE Euler step and then applies a stochastic Langevin correction targeting the marginal distribution at the resulting timestep. The required score is recovered directly from the flow velocity, requiring no additional score model, while the resulting transition remains an isotropic Gaussian with a tractable likelihood for policy optimization. We theoretically show that, under suitable conditions, one Langevin correction step reduces the Wasserstein error of an imperfect ODE Euler step. At a matched randomness level, we further show that the proposed transition can be more accurate than the standard Euler--Maruyama discretization of the reverse SDE. Experiments on SD3.5-Medium, FLUX.1-Dev, and HunyuanVideo demonstrate that LC-GRPO consistently improves reward optimization across text-to-image and text-to-video tasks, preserves generation quality, and substantially narrows the gap between stochastic training rollouts and deterministic test-time ODE inference.

</details>


### [199] [GAUGE: Granularity-Adaptive Counterfactual Gating of Evidence for Incomplete Multimodal Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05608)
*Yunping Shi,En Yu,Kairui Guo,Jie Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GAUGE 是一种轻量级反事实门控框架，针对不完整多模态分类问题，通过细粒度证据单元级别的泰勒评分与连续门控机制，在单次前向-反向传播中实现精准的证据调制，在多个基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态分类方法在处理模态缺失时，通常在粗粒度的模态级别进行插补和动态融合，无法在同一恢复模态内部保留可靠成分、抑制误导成分，从而损害预测可靠性。因此需要一种能够在细粒度证据单元层面进行选择性调制的框架。

Method: GAUGE 框架包含三个核心步骤：(1) 使用冻结的插补器补全缺失模态，将观测和恢复输入统一编码为细粒度证据单元；(2) 通过预测感知的泰勒证据评分，在单次前向-反向传播中计算每个单元被参考表示替换后的反事实效应；(3) 将评分映射为连续门控值，转化为加性注意力-logit偏置，在不改变骨干架构的前提下实现逐单元证据调制。同时提供泰勒余项理论分析，刻画一阶近似相对于精确反事实效应的误差界。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上的实验表明，GAUGE 在多种不完整输入设置下均显著优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: GAUGE 为模态不完整条件下的细粒度证据控制提供了一个有理论保障且可扩展的框架，其轻量级设计和架构无关的特性使其具备良好的实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Multimodal classification typically assumes all modalities are available, yet real-world inputs are often incomplete. Imputation and dynamic fusion can mitigate such incompleteness, but existing methods operate at a coarse modality level and thus cannot retain reliable components while suppressing misleading ones within the same recovered modality, compromising prediction reliability. To address this issue, we propose GAUGE, a lightweight counterfactual gating framework for incomplete multimodal classification. GAUGE first imputes missing modalities with a frozen imputer and encodes observed and recovered inputs uniformly as fine-grained evidence units. Rather than intervening on each unit explicitly, GAUGE scores the counterfactual effect of replacing every unit with a reference representation through prediction-aware Taylor evidence scores, all obtained in a single forward-backward pass. These scores are mapped to continuous gates, which are converted into additive attention-logit biases for unit-wise evidence modulation without altering the backbone architecture. Experiments across six benchmarks demonstrate that GAUGE outperforms strong baselines across diverse incomplete-input settings. Furthermore, a Taylor remainder theoretical analysis characterizes the error of the first-order approximation relative to the exact counterfactual effect, establishing GAUGE as a principled and scalable framework for fine-grained evidence control under modality incompleteness.

</details>


### [200] [Reasoning Errors Have a Region and a Direction in the Residual-Stream Trajectory of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05660)
*Hamed Damirchi,Ignacio Meza De la Jara,Damith Ranasinghe,Yuhang Liu,Javen Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种三流检测器，通过结合残差流位移（运动）与两种受限的位置视图（基于向量量化的粗粒度区域读取器和基于归一化多层状态的细粒度方向读取器），在不完全恢复完整状态的前提下为运动轨迹补充足够的上下文，从而在未见的推理基准上将选择准确率提升至多12%（相较仅用位移的SOTA）和21%（相较单层探测基线）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于轨迹的方法仅利用层级残差流位移来检测推理正确性，但位移丢弃了更新前的状态信息；而恢复完整状态又会重新引入依赖捷径的虚假相关性。作者识别出这一"位移-状态"权衡，旨在设计一种既保留足够状态上下文以解释运动、又避免完全回到全状态探测的检测方案。

Method: 提出三流检测器架构：(1) 运动流：继续使用残差流位移捕捉表征变化；(2) 区域流：基于向量量化的粗粒度区域读取器，将状态映射到离散码本，提供"模型处于哪个区域"的信息；(3) 方向流：对归一化后的多层状态进行细粒度方向读取。三个信号流互补，共同用于区分正确推理与错误推理。训练仅在推理基准上进行，测试时泛化到未见过的推理任务以及事实补全和事实验证任务。

Result: 在未见的推理基准上，该方法相较仅用位移的SOTA提升最高12%的选择准确率，相较单层探测基线提升21%。尽管仅在推理基准上训练，该方法在事实补全和事实验证任务上也优于所有对比检测器。消融实验表明运动、区域和方向三个信号流具有互补性。

Conclusion: 推理有效性的检测更应依赖于"以状态为条件的运动"（state-conditioned motion），而非单独的静态状态或去上下文的轨迹。三流设计在保持位移方法优势的同时，通过受限的状态视图恢复了足够的上下文信息，且信号指向的是"正确性"本身而非特定推理类型，具有良好的泛化性。

Abstract: As language models are increasingly used for tasks that require verifiable reasoning, reliably distinguishing sound reasoning from flawed reasoning has become an important practical problem. Recent trajectory-based methods seek this signal in layerwise residual-stream displacements, which capture how representations change while attenuating some stable, token-specific information. However, displacement omits the state from which an update originates, whereas restoring the full state risks reintroducing shortcut-prone information. We identify this trade-off and propose a three-stream detector that combines motion with two restricted views of location. A coarse region reader based on vector quantization and a fine direction reader over normalized multi-layer states. This design restores enough state context to interpret the motion without returning to full-state probing. On reasoning benchmarks unseen during training, our method improves selection accuracy by up to 12% over the displacement-only state of the art and 21% over single-layer probing baselines. Although trained only on reasoning benchmarks, it also reads factual completion and fact verification, ahead of every detector we compare against, which places the signal on correctness rather than on a kind of reasoning. Ablations further show that motion, region, and direction provide complementary signals. These results suggest that reasoning validity is better read from state-conditioned motion than from either static states or decontextualized trajectories alone.

</details>


### [201] [Potential Matching Optimal Transport: Continuous Normalizing Flows for Exact $p$-Wasserstein Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05666)
*Lishuo Zhang,Ruizhi Huang,Yang Yu,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PMOT是一种用于一般p-cost最优传输的势流框架，通过标量势参数化CNF速度场，利用自诱导匹配损失训练，零损失解可精确恢复p-最优传输映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优传输方法通常局限于2-cost (Wasserstein-2) 或依赖复杂对偶训练，缺乏适用于一般p-cost最优传输的统一框架，且难以在保证精确恢复的同时实现灵活的终端分布匹配。

Method: 提出PMOT框架：(1) 用标量势在广义Benamou-Brenier形式中参数化CNF速度场；(2) 设计自诱导匹配损失，沿模型自身端点决定的直线桥进行训练；(3) 允许灵活的终端分布匹配（如MMD）。理论上证明在正则性、精确终端匹配和唯一性假设下，零损失解满足广义Benamou-Brenier最优性系统并恢复p-最优传输映射。

Result: 在合成基准上，PMOT学到的p-特定映射与对应p-matched OT参考一致；在高维表格数据上作为基于似然的密度模型保持竞争力；基于MMD的颜色转换实验展示了灵活的样本级终端匹配能力。

Conclusion: PMOT为一般p-cost最优传输提供了统一的势流框架，兼具理论精确性和实践灵活性，在多个任务上表现优异。

Abstract: We introduce Potential Matching Optimal Transport (PMOT), a potential-flow framework for general $p$-cost optimal transport with $c_p(x,y)=\|x-y\|^p$. PMOT parameterizes the CNF velocity field with a scalar potential in the generalized Benamou--Brenier form for the chosen exponent $p$. It trains the potential gradient with a self-induced matching loss along straight bridges determined by the model's own endpoints, while allowing flexible terminal distribution matching. Our main result establishes zero-loss exactness: under the stated regularity, exact terminal matching, and uniqueness assumptions, any zero-loss solution satisfies the generalized Benamou--Brenier optimality system and recovers the corresponding $p$-optimal transport map and dynamics. On synthetic benchmarks, PMOT learns $p$-specific maps that agree with the corresponding $p$-matched OT references. It also remains competitive as a likelihood-based density model on high-dimensional tabular data, and an MMD-based color transformation experiment demonstrates flexible sample-based terminal matching.

</details>


### [202] [When Does Consensus Mean Correctness? Measuring the Agreement-Accuracy Coupling with Semantics-Preserving Re-Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05670)
*Rasul Khanbayov,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RENDEQ，通过程序化重绘科学图表生成语义等价的渲染等价集，定量测量了VLMs中"预测一致性"与"正确性"之间的耦合关系。核心发现：重渲染在准确性和可靠性上优于重采样；一致性在错误足够分散时才能作为可靠性信号；用模型自身跨渲染一致性进行微调会导致准确率下降，与自然图像上的已发表结果符号相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然图像扰动下，"模型在扰动输入间的一致性"与"正确性"之间的耦合关系很少被直接测量，因为自然图像扰动仅靠假设保持语义，且缺乏精确答案来定位错误。科学图表天然规避了这两个障碍：图表由程序从数据生成，重绘产生的图像在构造上语义等价，并共享程序精确答案。

Method: 构建RENDEQ生成器，通过控制渲染风格因子（如绘图库、颜色映射等）对同一数据程序化重绘，产生渲染等价图像集。在三款开源视觉语言模型上测量一致性-正确性耦合，所有发现均在三次独立实例化中交叉验证。对比重渲染与重采样的效果，将一致性信号与均值token对数概率基线比较，并分析各风格因子对分散度的贡献。最后用模型自身的跨渲染一致性作为微调目标，进行五次重复实验。

Result: 重渲染在准确性和可靠性上均优于重采样。一致性在两款模型上优于证据携带基线（均值token对数概率），在第三款上持平——此前一个因渲染管线故障导致的错误复现被纠正。分散度集中于绘图库这一风格因子，其影响是次大因子的两倍以上，比噪声基底高一个数量级。用跨渲染一致性微调导致所有五次重复实验中准确率下降，与自然图像上的已发表结果方向相反。一致性仅在模型错误足够分散（超过阈值）时才能认证正确性，而奖励一致性的目标恰恰破坏了这种分散性。

Conclusion: 预测一致性作为可靠性信号的有效性存在条件性：仅在模型错误分布足够分散时成立。以一致性为优化目标会产生适得其反的效果——它消除了错误分散性这一前提条件。RENDEQ提供的渲染等价框架为精确测量这一耦合提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: A model's agreement across perturbed inputs is used both as a label-free reliability signal and as a self-training target, on the premise that agreement tracks correctness. That coupling is rarely measured directly: natural-image perturbations preserve meaning only by assumption, and no exact answer key localizes errors. Scientific figures remove both obstacles, a figure is drawn from data by a program, so redrawing it yields images that are semantically equivalent by construction and share a programmatically exact answer. We build RENDEQ, a generator of such render-equivalence sets, and measure the coupling on three open-weight VLMs, checking every finding across three independent instantiations. Re-rendering beats resampling on both accuracy and reliability. Agreement beats an evidence-carrying baseline, mean token log-probability, on two of three models and ties on the third, reversing an intermediate, buggy replication traced to a rendering-pipeline failure. The dispersion behind this is concentrated in one style factor, the plotting library, more than double the next-largest factor and an order of magnitude above the noise floor. Fine-tuning on the model's own cross-render consensus inverts: accuracy falls in every one of five replication runs, the opposite sign to published results on natural images. Agreement certifies correctness only above a threshold set by how diffuse a model's errors are, and an objective that rewards agreement destroys exactly that diffuseness.

</details>


### [203] [Consistency Has a Computable Blind Spot: A Commutation Theory of Label-Free Reliability for Vision-Language Figure Reading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05675)
*Rasul Khanbayov,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了视觉-语言模型基于不变性的无标签可靠性评估存在一个可计算盲区：系统性误读可在扰动下存活并被错误认证。作者证明，当编辑与错误交换（commute）时，错误对编辑不可见，因此一套编辑无法捕获的错误构成其联合中心化子。基于对偶关系——等变性，作者提出等变性-一致性评分（ECS），并发布REND-EQUIV数据集。实验表明，循环重标记可弥补标签置换编辑的不完备性，且错误可检测性是关系与故障类的联合属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于不变性的无标签可靠性评估方法存在盲区：某些系统性误读可以在输入扰动下保持不变，从而被错误地认证为正确。作者旨在从数学上刻画这一盲区的来源，并提出一种基于等变性的互补方法来检测这些隐藏错误。

Method: 作者将不变性检测的盲区形式化为编辑与错误的交换子（commutator）问题：错误对编辑不可见当且仅当二者交换，所有不可见错误构成编辑簇的联合中心化子。基于对偶关系，提出等变性原理：编辑数据后正确答案应以可计算量改变。证明两种匹配编辑对仿射阅读错误是完备的，而交换编辑簇对标签置换不完备，循环重标记可弥补大部分缺口。在此基础上构建等变性-一致性评分（ECS），并发布配对不变性与等变性测试集的REND-EQUIV数据集。

Result: 在三类模型及手工标注群体上，预测排序成立；第二种不变性家族方法确认盲区属于关系本身而非实现；循环重标记在匹配真实样本上实现了预测增益。该理论框架还解释了分类器蜕变测试文献中排序反转现象：可检测性是关系与故障类的联合属性，而非关系的单独属性。

Conclusion: 基于不变性的可靠性评估存在可计算盲区，该盲区可通过等变性方法互补覆盖。错误可检测性本质上是编辑关系与故障类之间的联合属性，不能由编辑关系单独决定。循环重标记为标签置换提供了一个有效的补充策略。

Abstract: Label-free reliability for vision-language models rests on invariance: perturb the input and a faithful reader's answer should not change. This has a known blind spot, a systematic misreading survives the perturbation and gets certified wrong, which we show is computable, not just real: an error is invisible to an edit exactly when the two commute, so the errors a suite cannot reach form its joint centralizer, a set that shrinks as edits are added and can be written down rather than guessed at. We act on the complementary relation, equivariance: edit a figure's data and the correct answer must change by a computable amount. Two matched edits are provably complete for affine reading errors; no suite of swap edits is complete for label permutations, and cyclic relabeling closes most of that gap. We instantiate the theory as the Equivariance-Consistency Score, a label-free, training-free detector, and release REND-EQUIV, pairing matched invariance and equivariance sets over identical data. The predicted ordering holds across three models and a hand-labeled population immune to the one circularity in how it is selected; a second invariance-family method confirms the blind spot belongs to the relation, not to any implementation; and cyclic relabeling delivers its predicted gain on a matched real sample. The same characterization explains a reported inversion of this ordering in the classifier metamorphic-testing literature: detectability is a joint property of the relation and the fault class, never of the relation alone.

</details>


### [204] [SEAM: Global consistency beyond local accuracy in scientific machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05702)
*Gnankan Landry Regis N'guessan,Bum Jun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SEAM 框架将科学机器学习中局部解释的全局一致性检验转化为可计算问题，通过 Explanation-Sheaf 结构比较相邻区域的解释，将不一致性定位到具体通道，支持精确修复或残差感知的经验归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前科学机器学习仅在子域、基准划分或单预测解释层面进行局部验证，无法判断局部解释能否拼装成全局可接受的一致科学解释。缺乏从局部到全局的、跨区域/传感器/模态/模型组件的解释一致性审计工具。

Method: 提出 SEAM（Scientific Explanation-Admissibility Machines）生成器无关框架，其有限实例化 SEAM-Ω 为每个区域构建含状态、闭包、观测三通道及合约元数据的结构化解释；在相邻区域重叠部分比较解释，将分歧转化为通道分辨的 obstruction；支持精确最小代价修复（定理给出可行性条件）与残差感知正则化经验归因；可分离不一致性与非可识别性，监控分布偏移下的生成器。

Result: 在 19 项合成 PDE 系统与分布外 FNO 监控实验中，SEAM 在局部预测准确时仍能检测出不兼容解释，并将失效归因到特定通道与重叠区域。证明了最小代价干预定理与守恒-合约可检测性定理，以及可识别性与闭包可恢复性的配套结论。

Conclusion: SEAM 为现有求解器与学习模型增加了全局解释一致性审计能力，可检验其局部解释是否构成连贯的科学叙述，填补了从局部验证到全局一致性检验的空白。

Abstract: Scientific machine learning commonly validates models at the level of a subdomain, a benchmark split, or an explanation for one prediction. Yet such local checks cannot establish whether the resulting explanations can be assembled into one globally admissible explanation. We introduce Scientific Explanation-Admissibility Machines (SEAM), a generator-agnostic framework that makes this local-to-global consistency question computable across regions, sensors, regimes, and model components. The finite explanation-sheaf instantiation SEAM-$Ω$ represents each region by a structured explanation with state, closure, and observation channels together with optional contract metadata; compares neighboring explanations on their overlaps; and converts disagreement into a channel-resolved obstruction. This obstruction locates inconsistency and tests competing declared accounts by restricting each repair to the revisions that one account permits. Exact feasibility refutes or retains an account; when exact repair is unavailable, residual-aware regularized records provide a separately labeled empirical attribution. The framework also separates inconsistency from non-identifiability and monitors learned generators under distribution shift. We establish theorems for minimum-cost intervention and conservation-contract detectability, together with companion results for identifiability and closure recoverability. Across nineteen experiments involving synthetic partial differential equation systems and out-of-distribution Fourier neural operator (FNO) monitoring, SEAM detects incompatible explanations even when local predictions are accurate, and attributes failures to specific channels and overlaps. SEAM adds a global explanation-consistency audit to existing solvers and learning models, testing whether their local explanations form a coherent scientific account.

</details>


### [205] [Spectral Aliasing Pretext: A novel task for Self-Supervised fault diagnosis in rotating machinery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05705)
*Victor Gialis,Maxime Metz,David Esteve,Abdenour Soualhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为SAP的自监督预训练方法，通过故意欠采样制造频谱混叠，再训练Transformer重建原始频谱，从而在无标签振动数据上学习故障诊断所需的频域不变特征。实验表明，SAP配合线性探测仅需少量标签即可达到高分类性能，且优于全监督训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场景中深度学习用于机械故障诊断面临标注数据稀缺的困境。现有自监督方法常依赖可能破坏信号特征的数据增强，不适用于振动信号。需要一种无需标签、且不依赖破坏性增强的预训练方法，从无标签振动数据中提取面向故障诊断的有效表征。

Method: 提出SAP（Spectral Aliasing Pretext）自监督预训练方法。核心思路：对原始振动信号进行故意欠采样，使其频谱发生混叠折叠；然后训练一个Transformer模型，以混叠频谱为输入，以重建原始未折叠频谱为预训练任务。该前置任务迫使模型学习机械故障特有的频域不变特征，整个过程无需任何标签，也无需对原始信号进行破坏性数据增强。

Result: 在CWRU数据集上，SAP学习到的表征具有高度稳定性和判别力。线性探测（linear probing）设置下，仅使用极少比例标注数据即可快速达到极高的分类准确率，且方差很低。相比之下，全量微调甚至全监督训练反而无法获得更稳定或更优的结果。

Conclusion: SAP结合线性探测在标注数据有限时，比全监督训练更有效、更可靠，为工业场景下小样本机械故障诊断提供了一种更优的范式。

Abstract: Deep learning is a new way for machinery fault diagnosis but requires extensive labeled data, a scarce resource in industrial settings.
