Result: 在Buchwald-Hartwig、Pd催化BH偶联、专有HTE C-N偶联和酰胺形成等多个产率预测基准上，RxnCLF一致优于图和序列基线方法，R²指标提升，整体性能最优。

Conclusion: CRG-based RxnCLF作为可扩展的反应基础模型前景广阔，有潜力泛化至更广泛反应空间，支持区域选择性预测、对映选择性预测和反应条件优化等多种下游反应信息学任务。

Abstract: Reaction yield prediction remains challenging because labeled data are scarce and reaction space is both combinatorially large and sparsely populated, limiting the generalization of existing reaction representations. String-, fingerprint-, and graph-based reaction encodings only partially capture chemical transformations, making accurate prediction difficult for reactions with complex substrates. We propose reaction contrastive learning foundation (RxnCLF), a self-supervised contrastive framework for reaction representation learning. RxnCLF is built on a condensed reaction graph (CRG) that unifies reactant and product information into a single graph, enabling the model to learn explicit and enriched transformation structure rather than disconnected graphs. Pretrained on 1.7 million Pistachio reactions, RxnCLF learns a compact and continuous latent space that captures both reaction-center features and broader side chain contexts, making it transformation-aware and chemically interpretable. Fine-tuned on multiple yield prediction benchmarks, including Buchwald-Hartwig, Pd-catalyzed BH coupling, and proprietary HTE C-N coupling and amide formation datasets, RxnCLF consistently outperforms graph and sequence-based baselines, improving R2 and achieving the best performance overall. Our results highlight the promise of CRG-based RxnCLF as a scalable reaction foundation model, with the potential to generalize across broader reaction spaces and support diverse downstream reaction informatics tasks, including regioselectivity prediction, enantioselectivity prediction, and reaction condition optimization.

</details>


### [240] [Hypothesis Testing with Conditional Queries: Learnability and the Value of Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06262)
*Zonghuan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究模型评估中自适应查询与非自适应查询的效率差异，在条件查询模型下证明：两分布类可区分当且仅当其成对条件概率有正分离；自适应策略与非自适应策略之间的查询复杂度差距为Θ(N²)，即交互可将所需测试次数减少二次因子，但不会产生指数级优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型评估中面临一个核心设计选择：是在观测任何响应之前固定所有测试（非自适应），还是根据早期响应选择后续测试（自适应）。理解这两种策略在查询复杂度上的根本差异，对于设计高效的评估方案至关重要。

Method: 在有限结果空间X（|X|=N）上建立条件查询模型，分析成对条件概率的分离性作为可学习性的充要条件；对任意T查询自适应策略，构造使用O(N²(T+log(1/ρ)))次成对查询的随机化非自适应过程，使其模拟轨迹与自适应轨迹在总变差距离上不超过ρ；同时构造匹配的下界族，证明自适应查询复杂度为常数而最优非自适应复杂度为Ω(N²)。

Result: （1）两分布类可区分当且仅当其成对条件概率存在正分离；当分离为零时，在任意有限查询预算下最优最坏情形误差恰为1/2。（2）非自适应过程可用O(N²(T+log(1/ρ)))次查询模拟任意T查询自适应策略，总变差距离在ρ内。（3）存在一族实例，自适应查询复杂度为常数，而非自适应查询复杂度为Ω(N²)，证明最坏情形固定误差下的自适应差距为Θ(N²)。

Conclusion: 交互可将模型评估所需的测试数量减少二次因子，但自适应评估中看似指数级的分支结构并未转化为指数级查询优势。这一结果为理解评估策略的理论极限提供了精确刻画。

Abstract: Model evaluations may fix all tests before observing any responses or select later tests using earlier responses. We study this choice in a conditional-query model on a finite outcome space $\mathcal{X}$ with $|\mathcal{X}|=N$. We first ask which pairs of distribution classes can be reliably distinguished. We then ask how many additional queries are required to match an adaptive tester when all queried events must be fixed in advance. We show that learnability holds if and only if the two classes have positive separation in their pairwise conditional probabilities. When this separation is zero, the optimal worst-case error is exactly $1/2$ at every finite query budget. For any $T$-query adaptive policy and any $ρ\in (0,1)$, we construct a randomized non-adaptive procedure using $O(N^2(T + \log(1/ρ)))$ pair queries chosen before any response is observed. Its simulated transcript is within $ρ$ in total variation of the adaptive transcript, uniformly over all distributions in the model. We also construct a matching family with constant adaptive query complexity and $Ω_\varepsilon(N^2)$ non-adaptive query complexity. Consequently, the worst-case fixed-error adaptivity gap is $Θ_\varepsilon(N^2)$. Thus interaction can reduce the required number of tests by a quadratic factor, but the apparent exponential branching of an interactive evaluation does not yield an exponential query advantage.

</details>


### [241] [BaKron: Efficient Quantization with Kronecker-Factored Hessians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06291)
*Johann Birnick,Rayan Saab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BaKron 是一种高效的神经网络量化算法，利用双侧 Kronecker 分解 Hessian 近似来捕获更丰富的曲率信息，通过反对角并行化和递归分治策略，将计算复杂度从 O(m²n²) 降至 O(mn(m+n))，同时保持与 GPTQ 相同的立方缩放特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GPTQ 风格的自适应舍入（adaptive rounding）仅使用来自输入激活的单侧信息，忽略了输出坐标间的相关性。双侧 Kronecker 分解 Hessian 近似（如 BoA 和 YAQA 所用）能捕获这些信息，但直接在向量化权重域应用 GPTQ 计算代价过高。因此需要一种高效求解器来利用更丰富的曲率信息，同时控制计算成本。

Method: 提出 BaKron 求解器，核心结合两种技术：(1) 反对角并行化（anti-diagonal parallelism），利用 Kronecker 结构实现并行处理；(2) 递归分治构造（recursive divide-and-conquer），将大规模权重矩阵分解为更小子问题逐步求解。该求解器对基础量化器和 Hessian 估计器均保持模块化设计。此外，论文还探讨了 BaKron 可使用的多种 Hessian 类型，并给出了高效计算这些 Hessian 的技术。

Result: 对于 m×n 权重矩阵，BaKron 将顺序步骤降至 O(m+n)，总工作量从 O(m²n²) 降至 O(mn(m+n))，与 GPTQ 的立方缩放相匹配，同时利用了更丰富的双侧曲率信息。实验评估验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: BaKron 成功在保持 GPTQ 级别计算效率的前提下，引入了双侧 Kronecker 分解 Hessian 所提供的更丰富几何信息，为神经网络量化提供了一种兼具精度与效率的实用方案。

Abstract: We accelerate a family of algorithms for neural network quantization whose geometry is informed by any Kronecker-factored approximation of the Hessian. GPTQ-style adaptive rounding typically uses one-sided information derived from input activations. Two-sided Kronecker-factored Hessian approximations can additionally capture correlations across output coordinates, but applying GPTQ directly in the vectorized weight domain is computationally expensive. Building on the two-sided adaptive-rounding formulation used by BoA and YAQA, we introduce BaKron, an efficient solver that combines anti-diagonal parallelism with a recursive divide-and-conquer construction. For an $m\times n$ weight matrix, BaKron uses $O(m+n)$ sequential steps while reducing the total work from $O(m^2n^2)$ to $O(mn(m+n))$. Thus, it matches the cubic scaling of GPTQ while exploiting richer curvature information. Moreover, BaKron is modular with respect to both the base quantizer and the Hessian estimator. We also provide practical benchmarks, consider a range of Hessians that BaKron can be called with, find an efficient technique to compute these Hessians, and evaluate the algorithm experimentally.

</details>


### [242] [On-Policy Self-Distillation without Any Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06296)
*Yijiang Li,Bingyang Wang,Yijun Liang,Yunjie Tian,Di Fu,Nuno Vasconcelos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: U-OPSD 是一种无监督的在线策略自蒸馏方法，仅依赖模型自身生成的内部一致性来构建伪标签，无需外部监督信号，即可实现对 LLM 的自我纠错与性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线策略（自）蒸馏方法仍依赖外部监督（如真实标签、环境反馈或更大模型的引导），未能实现真正的"自"蒸馏。作者旨在探索完全基于模型自身生成、无需任何外部信号的自我蒸馏方法。

Method: 提出 U-OPSD：1）对同一问题采样多条 rollout，通过多数投票在自一致性阈值下构建伪解；2）以最短伪解为条件构造教师分布；3）将该教师分布蒸馏到模型最长错误补全的前缀上，使模型在自信犯错处实现精准自我纠错。

Result: 在 AIME24/25、HMMT25、MATH500 和 AMC23 等基准上，U-OPSD 在 Qwen3 非思考模式下相较基模型提升 8.5%（4B）和 10.7%（8B），比 OPSD 分别平均高出 3.2% 和 2.3%；在思考模式下与 OPSD 持平或略优，并分别超越 GRPO 0.7% 和 1.1%。

Conclusion: U-OPSD 证明了仅利用模型自身的内部一致性即可实现有效的在线策略自蒸馏，在不依赖任何外部监督的条件下，能达到甚至超越有监督方法的性能，为 LLM 的后训练自我改进提供了新范式。

Abstract: On-policy (Self-)Distillation (OPD / OPSD) has shown strong potential for post-training large language models (LLMs). However, existing methods still rely heavily on external supervision, including ground-truth signals, environmental feedback, or guidance from larger models, and therefore fall short of genuine "self"-distillation. In this study, we show that on-policy self-distillation can be achieved using only a model's own generations via internal consistency. We propose Unsupervised On-Policy Self-Distillation (U-OPSD). U-OPSD first samples multiple rollouts and constructs a pseudo-solution by majority vote under a self-consistency threshold. It then conditions a teacher distribution on the shortest pseudo-solution and distills it into prefixes of the model's longest incorrect completion, allowing the model to correct itself precisely where it is confidently wrong. Across diverse benchmarks, base models, and training settings, U-OPSD consistently improves over the base models and matches or surpasses supervised methods with ground truth (GT), such as OPSD and GRPO. On AIME24, AIME25, HMMT25, MATH500, and AMC23, U-OPSD improves over the base model by 8.5% and 10.7% on Qwen3 non-thinking mode at the 4B and 8B scales, respectively, and outperforms OPSD by an average of 3.2% and 2.3%. In thinking mode, U-OPSD remains on par with OPSD, outperforming it by 0.9% at 4B and matching it at 8B, while surpassing GRPO by 0.7% and 1.1%, respectively.

</details>


### [243] [RRC: Unlocking Generative Reward Models in LLM Reinforcement Learning via Ranking-Based Reward Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06310)
*Chenglong Wang,Ziming Zhu,Yifu Huo,Bei Li,Qiaozhi He,Yan Ding,Xiaoyang Hao,Yuxin Gao,Tianhua Zhou,Xiaojia Chang,Tongran Liu,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于排序的奖励构建方法（RRC），通过将生成式奖励模型的比较性输出转化为适合强化学习的标量奖励信号，解决了生成式奖励模型在RL训练中的适配问题，并在聊天和推理基准上取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式奖励模型虽然响应排序能力强，但在强化学习中未能发挥潜力。核心问题在于：生成式奖励模型本质上是比较性的（输出相对偏好排名），而现有RL算法采用标量评分范式，二者存在根本性不匹配。

Method: 提出RRC方法，包含两种互补策略：（1）自竞争排序（self-competitive ranking），利用采样响应之间的相互比较来构建奖励；（2）锚点引导排序（anchor-guided ranking），通过少量参考响应实现可扩展的排序奖励构建。两者均从相对偏好排名中推导出有效的RL学习信号。

Result: 在开放式聊天和推理基准测试中，RRC显著改善了使用生成式奖励模型的RL训练效果，相较于现有奖励构建方法取得了一致性提升。

Conclusion: RRC成功弥合了生成式奖励建模与标量强化学习之间的范式鸿沟，使生成式奖励模型能够为RL提供更有效的学习信号，推动了奖励建模从判别式向生成式转变的实用化进程。

Abstract: Recent advances in reward modeling show a paradigm shift from discriminative reward models to generative reward models. However, despite their strong capabilities in response ranking, generative reward models have not realized their potential in reinforcement learning (RL). Our analysis reveals that this limitation arises from a mismatch between the comparative nature of generative reward modeling and the scalar scoring paradigm adopted by existing RL algorithms. To bridge this gap, we propose a Ranking-based Reward Construction (RRC) approach, which enables generative reward models to provide more effective RL learning signals by deriving rewards from relative preference rankings. RRC introduces two complementary strategies: self-competitive ranking, which exploits comparisons among sampled responses, and anchor-guided ranking, which enables scalable ranking-based reward construction with a small set of reference responses. Experiments across open-ended chat and reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that RRC substantially improves RL training with generative reward models, achieving consistent gains over existing reward construction approaches. Our code can be found at https://github.com/wangclnlp/RRC.

</details>


### [244] [CalibForge: Adversarial Solver Calibration for Scaling Learnable Terminal Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06352)
*Fanzhe Meng,Guoxin Chen,Jiale Zhao,Shuang Sun,Zhiyu Lin,Wayne Xin Zhao,Ruihua Song,Ji-Rong Wen,Kai Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CalibForge 是一个自主终端任务合成系统，通过对抗性求解器校准（多求解器校准和对比求解器校准）来生成难度适中的训练任务。两种校准策略均优于仅靠人工编写和验证的方法，模型在多个基准上取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练终端智能体需要可执行且可验证的任务，但仅凭可执行性验证无法揭示任务相对于特定求解器的行为特征。作者希望找到一种方法，能够生成处于"求解器相对可学习区间"内的任务——既非简单到无需学习，也非困难到无法学习。

Method: 提出 CalibForge 系统，包含两种对抗性求解器校准策略：(1) 多求解器校准，利用异构求解器池中的分歧来定位可学习区间；(2) 对比求解器校准，设定"强求解器通过/弱求解器失败"的对照关系。两者均以已验证的可解性为锚点，通过求解器行为反馈迭代修正候选任务。

Result: 使用 CalibForge 构建了 5,431 个校准终端任务。消融实验表明两种策略均优于仅编写验证或单一求解器反馈。在完整数据集上训练的模型在 Terminal-Bench 2.0 上达到 32.58% 和 47.57%，相比基座模型最大提升分别为：Terminal-Bench 2.0 提升 24.71 个百分点，SWE-bench Pro 提升 27.68 个百分点，Doc2Repo 提升 30.04 个百分点。

Conclusion: 求解器相对可学习性作为构建有效且可迁移的智能体训练数据目标具有实用价值，CalibForge 生成的校准任务能够为终端智能体提供高质量的监督信号。

Abstract: Training terminal agents requires executable and verifiable tasks that are not merely solvable, but appropriately challenging for learning. Executable validation establishes feasibility, yet does not reveal how a task behaves relative to a given solver setting. In this paper, we present CalibForge, an autonomous terminal-task synthesis system that uses verified solver behavior to revise candidate tasks through adversarial solver calibration. Multi-solver calibration targets disagreement within a heterogeneous solver pool, whereas contrastive solver calibration targets a designated strong-pass/weak-fail relation; both operationalize a solver-relative learnable zone anchored in demonstrated solvability. Using CalibForge, we construct 5,431 calibrated terminal tasks. Our ablations show that both strategies yield more effective supervision than authoring and validation alone or ordinary single-solver feedback. Models trained on the full collection achieve 32.58% and 47.57% on Terminal-Bench 2.0. The largest improvements over the corresponding base model reach 24.71 percentage points on Terminal-Bench 2.0, 27.68 points on SWE-bench Pro, and 30.04 points on Doc2Repo. Together, these results support solver-relative learnability as a practical target for constructing effective and transferable agent training data.

</details>


### [245] [An Optimal Agnostic PAC Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06363)
*Markus Engelund Mathiasen,Jian Qian,Nikita Zhivotovskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对具有有限VC维的二分类假设类，构造了一个学习器，在不可知PAC学习框架下达到了统计最优的风险界，其样本复杂度与Devroye等人给出的下界在普适常数意义上匹配，从而彻底解决了不可知PAC学习的样本复杂度问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不可知PAC学习中，已知的下界与已有上界之间在风险项上存在差距——下界中第一项为√(L*·d/n)，而已有上界中对应项为√(d/n)，缺少L*因子。本文旨在构造一个能达到与下界匹配的学习器，从而在任意固定的L*处彻底解决该开放问题。

Method: 作者通过构造一个显式的学习算法，利用VC维的有限性（d≥1），基于i.i.d.样本进行训练。算法设计使得输出的假设 ĥ 能以高概率(1-δ)满足所给的风险界，其中的关键是将风险界表达为两项之和：一项与√(L*(d+log(1/δ))/n)成正比，另一项与(d+log(1/δ))/n成正比。

Result: 所构造的学习器实现了如下风险界：对于任意0<δ≤1/2，以概率至少1-δ，L(ĥ) ≤ L* + 7·10⁸·[√(L*(d+log(1/δ))/n) + (d+log(1/δ))/n]。该上界在普适常数因子意义上与已知下界完全匹配。

Conclusion: 本文彻底解决了不可知PAC学习中样本复杂度关于普适常数的刻画问题，在每一个固定的L*处，所获得的上下界仅相差一个绝对常数因子。这标志着该经典问题的理论终结。

Abstract: Let $H\subseteq\{-1,+1\}^X$ be a class of finite VC dimension $d\ge1$. Writing $L$ for the binary risk and $L^*=\min_{h\in H}L(h)$, we construct a learner achieving the statistically optimal risk bound: from an i.i.d.\ sample of size $n$, for every $0<δ\le 1/2$, with probability at least $1-δ$, \[
  L(\widehat h)
  \le L^*+ 7\cdot10^8\left(
  \sqrt{\frac{L^*(d+\log(1/δ))}{n}}
  +\frac{d+\log(1/δ)}{n}
  \right). \] This settles the sample complexity of agnostic PAC learning up to universal constants at every fixed $L^*$, matching the lower bounds of Devroye, Györfi, and Lugosi [A Probabilistic Theory of Pattern Recognition, Springer, 1996].

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [246] [Improving the efficiency of infectious disease prevention trials using negative control outcome event times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05261)
*Ethan Ashby,Zhewei Zhang,Tanya P. Garcia,Ting Ye,Holly Janes,Bo Zhang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出在传染病预防试验中，通过调整阴性对照结局（NCO）事件时间来提升治疗效应估计精度的方法。由于病原体暴露是关键的预后因素但基线不可测，传统协变量调整收益有限。作者利用与主要结局共享暴露机制、但不受干预因果影响的NCO事件时间，在右删失框架下推导有效影响函数，构建了交叉拟合一步估计量，具有多重稳健性和渐近有效性。在HVTN 704/HPTN 085试验中，该方法使预防效力估计方差降低约27%，远优于基线协变量调整的2.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传染病预防随机试验中，病原体暴露是主要的预后因素但基线无法测量，导致传统基线协变量调整对估计精度提升有限。需要一种能够间接捕捉暴露信息的调整策略来提升治疗效应估计效率。

Method: 提出调整阴性对照结局（NCO）事件时间——该结局不受干预因果影响但与主要结局共享暴露机制。形式化NCO调整有效的假设条件，分析右删失对调整的复杂化影响。推导主要结局生存函数在右删失下的有效影响函数，构建交叉拟合一步估计量（cross-fitted one-step estimator），该估计量对冗余模型误设具有多重稳健性，且在冗余参数准确估计时达到渐近有效。

Result: 数值实验中，当NCO事件时间不具信息性时，该方法与基准方法表现相当；当NCO对主要结局预后性增强时，精度随之提升。在HVTN 704/HPTN 085试验应用中，调整细菌性STI感染时间（HIV-1感染的阴性对照结局）使预防效力估计方差降低约27%，而传统基线协变量调整仅降低约2.5%。

Conclusion: 在传染病预防试验中，调整阴性对照结局事件时间可显著提升治疗效应估计的精度，远优于传统基线协变量调整。该方法为暴露不可直接测量场景下的效率提升提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Baseline covariate adjustment can enhance the efficiency of randomized trials by improving precision of treatment effect estimates. However, the precision gain depends on how strongly the baseline covariates are prognostic for the primary outcome. In randomized trials of infectious disease prevention interventions (e.g., vaccines or passively administered antibodies), an individual's exposure to the pathogen is a leading prognostic factor but is rarely measurable at baseline. Hence, conventional covariate adjustment offers limited precision gain in prevention trials. We propose adjusting for a negative control outcome (NCO) event time, which is causally unaffected by the intervention but shares overlapping exposure mechanisms with the primary outcome. We formalize assumptions under which adjustment for the NCO event time is valid, and show that right-censoring of the NCO event time further complicates adjustment. We derive the efficient influence function for the treatment-arm-specific survivor function of the primary outcome when both the primary outcome and the NCO event time are right-censored, and use it to construct a cross-fitted, one-step estimator that is multiply robust to nuisance misspecification and asymptotically efficient when the nuisances are estimated accurately. In numerical experiments, our estimator compares comparably to benchmarks when the NCO event time is uninformative, and gains precision as the NCO event time is more prognostic for the primary outcome. We apply our method to HVTN 704/HPTN 085, a randomized, double-blinded trial of VRC01, a broadly neutralizing antibody against HIV-1. Adjusting for the time to a bacterial sexually transmitted infection --- a negative control outcome for HIV-1 acquisition --- reduced the estimated variance of the prevention efficacy estimate by approximately 27%, compared to roughly 2.5% for baseline covariate adjustment.

</details>


### [247] [Precision and Decisiveness as Goals: Reliable Sequential Hypothesis Testing with a Dual Stopping Criterion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05301)
*Eyal A. Kazin*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: DPitG 通过同时要求精度达标和结论确定来终止实验，大幅降低了 PitG 的高不确定率，同时保持零假阳性，优于 HDI+ROPE 的耦合式早停方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序假设检验中的早停规则若与决策标准耦合（如 HDI+ROPE），在样本不具代表性时易导致确认偏差和系统性假阳性；而完全解耦的方法（如 PitG）虽消除了该偏差，却在零假设为真时频繁产生非结论性结果。因此需要一种能兼顾结论确定性和统计精度的新方法。

Method: 提出 DPitG（Decisive Precision is the Goal）方法，要求同时满足两个条件才停止采样：(1) HDI 宽度达到预设精度目标 ω；(2) HDI 完全落在 ROPE 之外（拒绝零假设）或完全落在 ROPE 之内（接受零假设）。通过公平硬币仿真（ω=0.08, ROPE=0.5±0.05）对比 DPitG、PitG 和 HDI+ROPE 的表现，并给出闭式规划公式 N ∝ V/ω² 及在线交互式计算器。

Result: DPitG 将 PitG 的非结论率从 62% 降至 2%，中位样本量仅增加 5%，且保持零假阳性；HDI+ROPE 虽可达类似结论性水平，但以假阳性为代价。在所测 ω 范围内，DPitG 结论率始终高于 97%，而 PitG 波动较大。

Conclusion: DPitG 的完全预注册兼容的停止规则使其成为需要可靠结论场景的首选方法，该框架可轻松扩展至连续数据和两组比较。

Abstract: Sequential hypothesis testing offers flexibility over fixed-sample designs, but stopping rules coupled to decision criteria risk confirmation bias through early peeking. The HDI+ROPE algorithm exemplifies this: it stops as soon as the posterior Highest Density Interval (HDI) falls entirely outside a Region of Practical Equivalence (ROPE) around the null (rejecting it) or entirely inside, which enables positive acceptance of the null, something Null Hypothesis Significance Testing cannot do. However, this coupling means a wide HDI can satisfy the criterion on early, unrepresentative samples, incurring a systematic false-positive cost. Fully decoupled methods, such as ``Precision is the Goal'' (PitG), eliminate this bias by halting only once a target HDI width $ω$ is reached; yet, by divorcing the stopping rule from the decision criterion, PitG frequently yields inconclusive outcomes, particularly when the null hypothesis is true. We propose ``Decisive Precision is the Goal'' (DPitG), which requires both the precision target and a conclusive verdict to be satisfied simultaneously. In fair coin simulations ($ω=0.08$, ROPE$=0.5\pm0.05$), DPitG reduces the PitG inconclusive rate from 62% to 2% at a median cost of only 5% more samples, with zero false positives; HDI+ROPE achieves comparable conclusiveness only at the cost of false positives. Across the $ω$ range tested, DPitG conclusiveness remains above 97% while PitG's varies widely. We provide a closed-form planning formula $N\propto V/ω^2$ (where $V$ is the observation variance), an online interactive calculator, and open-source code. Demonstrated on single-group binary data, the framework extends readily to continuous outcomes and two-group comparisons. DPitG's fully pre-specified stopping rule is compatible with pre-registration standards and is the method of choice whenever a reliable verdict is required.

</details>


### [248] [Joint Spatial and Temporal Generalized Dissimilarity Mixed Modeling (stGDMM) for Beta Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05352)
*Philip A. White,Henry A. Frye,Jasper A. Slingsby,John A. Silander,,Hana Petersen,Alan E. Gelfand*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文扩展了空间广义相异性混合模型（spGDMM），将其推广至时空动态情境。通过将beta多样性（基于Bray-Curtis相异性度量）置于时空对的乘积空间上进行建模，作者利用南非开普植物区（CFR）的数据展示了该动态框架的丰富建模潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期的广义相异性模型（GDM）存在一些随机性方面的问题，spGDMM虽已对空间情形进行了补救，但缺乏对时间动态的考量。beta多样性本身蕴含空间和时间的双重变异信息，亟需一个能够同时捕捉时空动态的统一建模框架。

Method: 将原有的空间广义相异性混合模型（spGDMM）扩展为时空动态版本。模型将beta多样性（采用Bray-Curtis相异性）置于一对时空点对的乘积空间上进行建模，从而同时刻画物种组成随空间和时间的相异性变化。

Result: 在时空框架下发现了比纯空间模型更丰富的建模可能性。使用南非开普植物区（CFR）数据集进行了实证展示。

Conclusion: 考虑时间动态后，beta多样性建模可获得更为丰富的洞察。时空spGDMM为生物多样性的统计建模提供了更强有力的分析工具。

Abstract: Generalized dissimilarity models (GDMs) have emerged as a valuable tool for formal statistical analysis of biodiversity. In particular, beta diversity, measured using dissimilarity measures, e.g., the Bray-Curtis dissimilarity in our case, provides a statistical summary of the difference in species composition between sites. It also provides novel data for spatial and spatio-temporal modeling as it resides over the product space of one space-time pair and a second space-time pair. In earlier work we developed the spatial generalized dissimilarity mixed model (spGDMM) to remedy some of the stochastic issues concerned with the foundational GDM in the literature. Here, we extend that work to include dynamics. We find much richer modeling opportunities as we consider beta diversity with regard to change in time as well as space. We illustrate with a dataset from the Cape Floristic Region (CFR) in South Africa.

</details>


### [249] [Sparse Principal Component Analysis via Wavelets for Distributed Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05386)
*Giovanni Barbosa Herrero,Rodney Vasconcelos Fonseca,Aluísio Pinheiro*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将基于小波的稀疏化方法（Johnstone & Lu, 2009）融入分布式PCA框架（Fan et al., 2019）的新方法，旨在降低通信成本的同时保持特征空间估计质量。模拟实验表明，在超过一定维度阈值后，该方法在估计误差上优于Fan et al. (2019)，且传输系数系统性更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习场景下，数据分布在多台机器上，无法直接访问全样本。Johnstone & Lu (2009) 指出PCA在高维情形下统计不一致，并以小波稀疏化恢复一致性；Fan et al. (2019) 虽实现了分布式PCA的特征空间估计，但未处理高维情形。本文动机在于填补这一空白——将高维一致性的解决方案引入分布式框架，同时降低通信开销。

Method: 将Johnstone & Lu (2009) 的小波基稀疏化（wavelet-based sparsification）与变量选择技术，嵌入Fan et al. (2019) 的分布式PCA估计框架中，使得各机器仅需传输稀疏化后的小波系数，从而在分布式高维场景下估计汇聚数据的特征空间。

Result: 在维度 d ∈ [52, 5000] 的模拟实验中，所提方法在超过特定维度阈值（λ=25 时 d ≥ 152；λ=50 时 d ≥ 252）后，估计误差优于 Fan et al. (2019)，且在整个研究范围内系统性传输更少的系数。

Conclusion: 该方法成功在分布式高维PCA中实现了估计精度与通信效率的双重提升，验证了小波稀疏化在联邦学习场景中的有效性。

Abstract: The large volume of data and concerns about data privacy have motivated the development of techniques for distributed data, a problem also known as federated learning. In this scenario, sub-samples of the data are divided across different machines, and statistics must be computed over that data without direct access to the full sample. Johnstone & Lu (2009, JASA) show that principal component analysis (PCA) is statistically inconsistent in the high-dimensional regime, and propose a way to recover consistency through wavelet-based sparsification and variable selection. Fan et al. (2019, AoS) show a way to perform this same estimation -- specifically, to estimate the eigenspace that would be obtained if all the data were pooled together, even though it remains effectively distributed -- without addressing the high-dimensional regime. This work incorporates the wavelet-based sparsification of Johnstone & Lu (2009) into the distributed PCA framework of Fan et al. (2019), aiming to reduce communication cost without compromising the quality of the eigenspace estimation. Simulations across $d \in [52, 5000]$ show that the proposed method overtakes Fan et al. (2019) in estimation error beyond a clear dimensional threshold ($d \geq 152$ for $λ=25$, $d \geq 252$ for $λ=50$), while transmitting systematically fewer coefficients throughout the entire range studied. This study was financed by the Sao Paulo Research Foundation (FAPESP), Brazil. Process Number #2023/02538-0 and Number #2025/21250-2.

</details>


### [250] [Inference for subgraph densities in noisy dynamic networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05407)
*Peter W. MacDonald,Eric D. Kolaczyk*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对动态网络序列中子图密度的统计估计与推断方法，能显式校正边观测误差，并在网络规模增长时具有良好的理论性质。该方法基于隐马尔可夫网络模型，解决了非相同网络复制下的稳健性和多快照高效聚合两大问题，并扩展至多时间点子图密度的联合推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chang et al. (2022) 指出了两个有待进一步研究的重要方向：(1) 对非相同网络复制的稳健性，(2) 多个可用网络快照的高效聚合。动态网络数据中常会遇到这些挑战，现有方法在处理含噪网络时存在局限，亟需开发能同时应对观测误差和动态复制的统计方法。

Method: 作者构建了一个随机演化的隐马尔可夫网络模型（hidden Markov network model），在此基础上开发了子图密度的估计和推断方法。该方法能对网络边的观测误差进行显式校正，并支持多个网络快照的有效聚合。此外，方法被扩展至多时间点子图密度的联合推断，以便对动态网络快照进行正式的统计比较。

Result: 所提方法在网络规模增长时展现出良好的理论性质，能够有效处理含噪动态网络中的子图密度估计问题，并实现了对非相同网络复制的稳健推断以及多快照的高效信息聚合。

Conclusion: 该方法极大地扩展了含噪网络分析的数据适用范围，使网络复制在动态观测场景下也能得到有效处理，为动态网络快照的正式统计比较提供了可行框架。

Abstract: In this work we develop statistical methodology to estimate and perform inference on subgraph densities using time-indexed, or dynamic network sequences. These estimates explicitly adjust for observation errors for the network edges, and have good theoretical properties as the size of the network grows. By specifying a stochastically evolving hidden Markov network model, we address two important directions for further investigation identified by Chang et al. (2022): robustness to non-identical network replicates, and efficient aggregation of multiple available network snapshots. These new methods vastly expand the analysis of noisy networks to new data settings, as network replicates are commonly observed dynamically. The methodology is also extended to consider joint inference for subgraph densities at multiple time points, to facilitate formal statistical comparison of dynamic network snapshots.

</details>


### [251] [Risk-Aware Quantile Learning for Personalized Dynamic Treatment Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05434)
*Chunyin Lei,Annie Qu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出风险感知的分位数动态治疗方案（RQDTR）框架，在优化累积潜在结果预设分位数的同时显式纳入治疗相关风险，并通过角度公式扩展到多类别治疗决策。理论证明了识别等价性、Fisher一致性及有限样本误差率，模拟和实证表明RQDTR在尾部疗效和获益-风险权衡上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序贯临床决策不仅要最大化平均疗效，还需同时优化结局分布的临床相关尾部、控制治疗风险、处理多类别治疗选择。现有分位数DTR方法局限于仅考虑疗效目标和二元治疗，无法满足这些复合需求。

Method: 提出RQDTR统一框架，包含三个子类：(1)仅疗效分位数学习；(2)带群体水平风险控制的约束学习；(3)基于复合获益-风险效用的效用学习。采用角度公式实现多类别决策规则的联合学习。理论上建立了识别与oracle等价性、平滑替代函数的Fisher一致性、估计 regimes 的一致性及有限样本误差界。

Result: 模拟研究表明RQDTR在尾部导向疗效上优于现有分位数DTR方法，并在All of Us抑郁症和MIMIC-III脓毒症真实数据中展现出更优的获益-风险权衡。

Conclusion: RQDTR框架有效统一了尾部优化、风险控制和多类别决策，弥补了现有分位数DTR方法的局限，为临床决策提供了更灵活、更具实践价值的工具。

Abstract: Sequential clinical decision-making often involves more than maximizing average efficacy. Clinicians may need to simultaneously optimize clinically relevant tails of the outcome distribution, control treatment-related risk, and choose among multiple treatment options. Existing quantile dynamic treatment regime (DTR) methods capture distributional features of treatment outcomes but remain largely restricted to efficacy-only objectives and binary treatments. To address these limitations, we propose Risk-Aware Quantile Dynamic Treatment Regimes (RQDTR), a unified framework that optimizes a prespecified quantile of the cumulative potential outcome while explicitly incorporating treatment-related risk. We also develop an angle-based formulation for jointly learning decision rules across multiple treatment categories. Our framework includes three interpretable subclasses: efficacy-only quantile learning, constraint-based learning with population-level risk control, and utility-based learning through a composite benefit-risk utility. Theoretically, we establish identification and oracle equivalence, Fisher consistency of the smoothed surrogate, consistency of the estimated regime, and finite-sample performance error rates. Extensive simulation studies and applications to All of Us major depressive disorder and MIMIC-III sepsis data demonstrate that RQDTR improves tail-oriented efficacy and achieves more favorable benefit-risk trade-offs than existing quantile DTR methods.

</details>


### [252] [Fast Power Evaluation under Biased-Coin Minimization: Sampling and Randomization Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05527)
*Masahiro Kojima*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出可重用的层间不平衡高斯近似（SIGA）框架，将固定得分统计量精确分解为联合层不平衡与正交层内分量，从而大幅降低偏币最小化设计下功效与样本量评估的计算负担。SIGA-S 适用于边际均值/风险差边界，SIGA-R 则通过配对分配校准近似条件参考分布，两者均与参考随机化检验高度吻合，且计算效率显著优于嵌套重随机化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在偏币最小化设计中，若在每个模拟试验内重现预设的随机化检验来评估功效和样本量，计算成本极高。现有方法缺乏一种可重用、高效且能同时处理边际边界与非锐利边界下条件推断的近似框架。

Method: 作者提出 SIGA 框架：将固定得分统计量精确分解为联合层不平衡分量和与之正交的层内分量。基于显式分配-复制限制条件（相同绝对不平衡规则），开发两种校准程序——(1) SIGA-S（抽样校准）：在边际均值或风险差边界下一致估计重复抽样方差；(2) SIGA-R（随机化校准）：通过可重用的配对分配校准，利用二次型刻画一阶方差间隙（涉及配对路径协方差），近似条件参考分布。

Result: 独立综合基准测试显示，SIGA-S 和 SIGA-R 与对应的参考随机化检验结果高度吻合。基于已发表总体规划特征的试验模拟中，SIGA-S、SIGA-R 与参考随机化检验的功效相近，但两种可重用校准程序相较嵌套重随机化大幅降低了计算量。

Conclusion: SIGA 框架通过统计量分解和可重用校准策略，在不牺牲精度的前提下显著提升了偏币最小化设计下功效与样本量评估的计算效率，为临床试验设计提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Design-stage power and sample-size evaluation under biased-coin minimization can be computationally intensive when a prespecified randomization test is reproduced within every simulated trial. We develop a reusable stratum-imbalance Gaussian approximation (SIGA) framework by exactly decomposing a fixed-score statistic into joint-stratum imbalance and orthogonal within-stratum components. Under explicit allocation-copy limit conditions for the same absolute-imbalance rule, the sampling-calibrated procedure, SIGA-S, consistently estimates the repeated-sampling variance at a marginal mean- or risk-difference boundary. At a nonsharp boundary, the conditional variance of a fixed-score randomization test can differ because the score contains the observed allocation path. To characterize this distinction, we express the first-order variance gap as a quadratic form involving pair-path covariance and introduce the randomization-calibrated procedure, SIGA-R, based on a reusable paired allocation-only calibration to approximate the conditional reference distribution. Separate comprehensive benchmarks showed close agreement between each SIGA procedure and the corresponding reference randomization test. A trial-inspired simulation based on published aggregate planning characteristics likewise produced similar power for SIGA-S, SIGA-R and the reference randomization test, while both reusable calibration procedures substantially reduced computation relative to nested rerandomization.

</details>


### [253] [Two base rates, two weights: base-rate neglect has a second axis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05658)
*Adam Y. Shavit*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个双权重贝叶斯框架，指出传统意义上的"基率忽视"实际上包含两种独立错误：忽视先验概率（经典基率忽视）和忽视线索本身频率（线索密度效应）。作者论证两者可通过一个贝叶斯方程中的两个权重来刻画，并预测两者可被实验性地双重分离，这是单参数模型无法解释的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统研究将基率忽视视为单一错误（对先验概率赋予过低权重），但作者指出，从共现信息中形成有效判断需要同时校正两个基率：结果的先验概率和线索本身的频率。线索密度效应在偶联学习中已被长期研究，却未被识别为另一种基率忽视。作者旨在统一这两种忽视，揭示现有学习与记忆模型因实验范式限制而掩盖的分歧，并提出可检验的双重分离预测。

Method: 作者构建了一个统一的贝叶斯方程，将先验校正和线索频率校正分别表示为两个独立的权重参数。通过分析不同任务范式（分级评分 vs. 二择一测验），揭示线索频率权重仅在分级评分中可测量，而二择一测验会将其抵消。进一步将这一框架与信号检测论准则、线索效度指标（contiguity/sensitivity/validity）、因果强度"lift"量度，以及六种标准学习与记忆模型进行映射和对比。

Result: 该框架统一了多个既有概念：在权重极端取值下，可还原为经典基率忽视、信号检测论决策准则、线索效度三元组和lift因果强度量度。线索频率权重同时嵌入于六种标准学习与记忆模型中，这些模型看似一致，仅因传统实验将数据压缩至无法暴露分歧的形式。核心预测是两种忽视可被独立操纵（双重分离），而单参数模型无法产生这一结果。

Conclusion: 基率忽视应被重新理解为两种独立的校正错误，而非单一错误。该双权重贝叶斯框架的核心预测——两种忽视可被实验性地双重分离——尚未被检验，需要采用分级评分实验加以验证。本文提供了理论框架和实验设计方案，后续论文将用已有的颜色-味道数据集对模型进行拟合。

Abstract: Base-rate neglect is usually treated as one mistake: giving the prior too little weight. Turning the co-occurrences you see into a useful judgment, though, means correcting for two base rates, not one. The first is the familiar prior, how common the outcome is. The second is how common the cue itself is. Those are two separate mistakes, and a learner can make either one alone. Under-correcting the prior is classical base-rate neglect; under-correcting the cue is the cue-density effect of contingency learning, long studied but not previously recognised as a kind of base-rate neglect. We write both corrections as two weights in one Bayesian equation. The task decides which weight it can measure: the cue-frequency weight appears only in graded ratings, because a two-choice test cancels it. At their extremes the two weights recover familiar quantities: base-rate neglect, the signal-detection criterion, the contiguity/sensitivity/validity triple, and the "lift" measure of causal strength. The same cue-frequency weight also sits inside six standard learning-and-memory models; they seem to agree, but only because the usual experiments squeeze the data into a form where they cannot disagree. Above all, the two neglects should be separately manipulable: an experimenter can move one without moving the other. That is a double dissociation, and a one-parameter account cannot produce it. That prediction is the framework's centre, and it has not yet been tested. This paper lays out the framework and the rating experiment that would settle it; a companion paper fits the two weights to an existing colour-flavour dataset.

</details>


### [254] [Multiparametric MRI Radiomics and Machine Learning Framework for Predicting Treatment Response in Glioblastoma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05733)
*Suchibrata Patra*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本研究探索了基于DCE-MRI的简约体素级药代动力学模型放射组学特征联合MGMT状态，区分GBM放化疗后真性进展(TP)与假性进展(PsP)。随机森林分类器结合简约DCE-MRI放射组学与MGMT状态，AUC达0.89，显著优于单一模型(0.68)和T1增强基线(0.72)。体素级自适应模型选择能改善非侵入性鉴别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GBM患者放化疗后，真性进展(TP)与假性进展(PsP)在常规增强MRI上表现几乎一致，但两者治疗方案截然不同，临床鉴别需求迫切。现有常规影像无法可靠区分，亟需开发非侵入性、高准确率的鉴别方法。

Method: 纳入82例IDH野生型GBM成人患者，均在放化疗后6个月内出现新增强病灶。金标准：组织病理学(n=52)或改良RANO标准(n=30)分为53例TP、29例PsP。在每个体素上，将对比剂浓度-时间曲线拟合至5种候选药代动力学模型，通过AIC最小化选取最优模型，生成自适应的Ktrans、Ve、Vp、taui参数图。分割后提取1073个放射组学特征，经Mann-Whitney U筛选和Elastic Net降维，训练5种分类器、4种特征配置。

Result: 随机森林分类器联合简约DCE-MRI放射组学与MGMT状态表现最佳：平均AUC 0.89、敏感性0.93、特异性0.76、F1 0.90。优于无MGMT(0.84)、T1增强基线(0.72)和单一扩展Tofts模型(0.68)。Ktrans图的形状和纹理特征及肿瘤体积为最强预测因子，MGMT贡献较小但独立。

Conclusion: 基于体素级自适应药代动力学模型的放射组学分析联合MGMT状态，可有效非侵入性鉴别GBM放化疗后TP与PsP。允许模型在体素水平自适应变化是提升鉴别性能的关键创新，优于传统单一固定模型方法。

Abstract: Distinguishing True Progression (TP) from Pseudo-Progression (PsP) after chemoradiotherapy remains a major diagnostic challenge in GBM, as both entities present near-identical appearances on conventional contrast-enhanced post-treatment MRI. This distinction carries substantial clinical weight, since TP and PsP demand divergent management yet cannot be reliably separated on routine imaging alone. We investigated whether radiomic features derived from a parsimonious, voxel-wise pharmacokinetic model of dynamic contrast-enhanced (DCE) MRI, combined with MGMT status, could discriminate between the two. The cohort comprised 82 adults with IDH-wildtype GBM who developed a new contrast-enhancing lesion within six months of chemoradiotherapy; classification (53 TP, 29 PsP) was established by histopathology where available (n=52) and modified RANO criteria otherwise (n=30). At every voxel, contrast-concentration time courses were fitted to five candidate pharmacokinetic models, and the best fit was retained by AIC minimisation, yielding parsimonious Ktrans, Ve, Vp, and taui maps adapting to local heterogeneity rather than a single fixed model across the tumour. Following segmentation, 1,073 radiomic descriptors were extracted and reduced via Mann-Whitney U filtering and Elastic Net, then used to train five classifiers across four feature configurations. A Random Forest classifier combining parsimonious DCE-MRI radiomics with MGMT status achieved the best discrimination (mean AUC 0.89, sensitivity 0.93, specificity 0.76, F1 0.90), outperforming features without MGMT (0.84), a T1-post-contrast baseline (0.72), and a single-model extended-Tofts analysis (0.68). Shape and textural descriptors of the Ktrans map, with tumour volume, were the strongest predictors, MGMT contributing a smaller, independent effect. Allowing the model to vary voxel-wise improves non-invasive discrimination.

</details>


### [255] [Fuzzy network jump models for soft dynamic clustering of graph-structured data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05786)
*Federico P. Cortese*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种模糊网络跳跃模型，对加权图节点上时变观测进行聚类，通过空间与时序正则化实现平滑软聚类分配，利用交替优化估计参数，在模拟和旧金山交通网络数据中表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的时变数据往往依附于图结构（如交通网络中相连路段的车流），需要一种既能捕捉图节点间空间依赖、又能平滑追踪聚类演化趋势的聚类方法，而现有方法缺乏同时兼顾空间与时序正则化的模糊聚类框架。

Method: 提出模糊网络跳跃模型（fuzzy network jump model），在加权图上构建软聚类分配，并引入空间正则化项（鼓励相邻节点的聚类隶属度接近）和时间正则化项（鼓励连续时间点隶属度平滑变化）。估计采用交替优化策略，充分利用正则化项的二次结构高效求解。

Result: 模拟研究表明，该方法在不同空间依赖程度和簇重叠度下均能准确恢复真实隶属概率，且优于其他竞争聚类方法。在旧金山交通网络数据中，成功识别出可解释的交通状态（如拥堵、畅通等），并揭示了这些状态随时间和跨路段的空间演化模式。

Conclusion: 所提出的模糊网络跳跃模型能够有效对图节点上时变数据进行聚类，兼顾空间平滑性与时间连续性，具有优越的聚类精度和实际应用价值。

Abstract: We introduce a fuzzy network jump model for clustering time-varying observations indexed by the nodes of a weighted graph. The framework allows flexible graph representations with spatial and temporal regularization promoting smooth soft cluster assignments across connected nodes and consecutive time points. Estimation is performed through an efficient alternating optimization scheme that exploits the quadratic structure of the regularization terms. A simulation study covering different levels of spatial dependence and cluster overlap shows that the proposed method accurately recovers the true membership probabilities and outperforms competing clustering methods. An application to traffic-network data for the city of San Francisco identifies interpretable traffic regimes and reveals their evolution over time and across connected road segments.

</details>


### [256] [Joint Multiple Imputation of Node Attributes and Network Ties in R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05895)
*Robert W. Krause*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: netimpute 是一个 R 包，通过链式方程联合填补社会网络中的缺失节点属性与缺失网络关系，利用网络结构信息预测属性、利用属性同质性预测关系，并在每次缺失关系更新时使用序贯 Gibbs 步骤实时刷新内生网络统计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缺失数据处理方法存在割裂：通用多重插补软件（如 mice）无法利用网络结构信息，而专门的网络插补程序又将关系插补与属性插补视为独立问题，缺乏统一框架来联合处理两类缺失。

Method: 提出 netmice() 链式方程插补框架：用网络衍生的结构/同质性主成分预测缺失节点属性，用属性衍生的同质性及其他网络衍生的预测因子填补缺失关系；关系填补采用逐对序贯 Gibbs 抽样，每次抽取后通过变更统计量实时更新互惠性和共同联系人等内生统计量。包还支持结构零、跨网络逻辑约束、社会关系模型随机截距、自定义交互项模型以及网络感知的预测变量筛选（netquickpred()）。

Result: 该包能计算二值和加权网络上的广泛节点级结构与属性同质性测度，并通过主成分降维生成可管理的预测变量集，同时可选择保留用于假设检验的特定原始测度。

Conclusion: netimpute 填补了网络数据多重插补的方法论空白，将属性插补与关系插补纳入统一的链式方程框架，为网络研究中缺失数据问题提供了一个整合、灵活且统计上合理的解决方案。

Abstract: Missing data in social network studies routinely affects both node-level attributes and the network ties themselves. Standard multiple imputation software such as mice (van Buuren and Groothuis-Oudshoorn, 2011) handles the former well but has no notion of network structure, while dedicated network-imputation procedures typically treat tie imputation and attribute imputation as separate problems. This paper introduces netimpute, an R package that (a) computes a broad battery of node-level structural and attribute homophily measures across one or more networks, (b) can reduce these to a manageable set of principal component predictors while optionally preserving specific raw measures that are themselves part of substantive hypotheses, (c) provides a dyadic (cell-level) regression for network ties in the spirit of MR-QAP (Krackhardt, 1988), and (d) combines both into netmice(), a chained-equations routine that jointly imputes missing node attributes and missing network ties by cycling between them, using network-derived predictors for attributes and attribute-derived (and other-network-derived) predictors for ties. Network ties are updated tie-wise by default: a sequential Gibbs step redraws each missing tie one at a time, conditional on all previously imputed ties, refreshing the endogenous statistics (reciprocity, shared contacts) after every single draw via change statistics. The package additionally supports structural zeros, logical constraints between networks, social-relations-model random intercepts in the tie model, custom imputation models with interactions among any internally created terms, and network-aware per-target predictor selection (netquickpred()). We describe the package's design choices, the measures it computes for binary and non-negative weighted networks, and illustrate its use with a reproducible example

</details>


### [257] [A Regression-Based Framework for the ACF, PACF, Durbin-Levinson Recursion, and One-Step-Ahead Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06334)
*Kellen Gong,Fang Li*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一的、基于回归的教学框架，将自相关函数（ACF）、偏自相关函数（PACF）、Durbin-Levinson递推和一步超前预测串联起来，展示它们本质上就是普通最小二乘回归中的系数，从而降低回归课程与时间序列课程之间的概念壁垒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的ACF和PACF教学通常以计算程序的方式呈现，与学生已有的回归分析知识脱节。作者旨在弥合这一鸿沟，让学生在熟悉的回归框架下理解这些时间序列核心工具的内在联系。

Method: 将ACF重新解释为平稳过程对其自身滞后项进行简单线性回归的系数；将PACF解释为不断扩展的多元回归模型中最新滞后项的系数；将Durbin-Levinson递推视为一系列偏回归，每一步都可用普通最小二乘恒等式表达。通过MA(1)和AR(1)的实例演示ACF和PACF的拖尾与截尾行为如何自然地从该视角产生。

Result: 在AR(1)和MA(1)过程的示例中，ACF和PACF的拖尾与截尾特征直接从回归框架中推导出来，验证了该教学框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 以回归为统一语言的框架能有效降低时间序列分析的学习门槛，作者建议在课堂教学中采用这一视角，并提供了相应的教学建议。

Abstract: The autocorrelation function (ACF) and partial autocorrelation function (PACF) are foundational tools for identifying autoregressive moving-average (ARMA) models, yet they are often introduced to students as computational recipes disconnected from the regression framework students already know. This note develops a unified, regression-based instructional framework for the ACF, the PACF, the Durbin-Levinson recursion, and one-step-ahead prediction in stationary time series. We show that the ACF is precisely the coefficient from a simple linear regression of a stationary process on one of its own lags, that the PACF is the coefficient on the newest lag in an expanding multiple regression model, and that the Durbin-Levinson recursion is nothing more than a sequence of partial regressions, each expressible using ordinary least-squares identities from a first course in regression. Worked examples for the MA(1) and AR(1) processes illustrate how the tailing-off and cutting-off behavior of the ACF and PACF emerge directly from this viewpoint. We argue that this framing lowers the conceptual barrier between regression courses and time series courses and provide classroom-oriented suggestions for instruction.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [258] [Quality Diversity for Reliable Data Driven Time-Use Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05230)
*Aneta Neumann,Ty Stanford,Dorothea Dumuid,Frank Neumann*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对有限24小时时间分配的日常活动构成与身心健康指标之间的关系，提出了一种融入预测不确定性的质量多样性（QD）优化框架，利用大规模儿童队列数据（n>1000）进行成分数据分析，生成兼顾预期健康收益与模型置信度的更可靠时间使用建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间使用优化方法仅关注最大化预期健康收益，忽视了数据驱动预测中固有的不确定性，这可能导致不切实际的时间使用建议。因此，需要将预测不确定性纳入优化过程，以提升推荐结果的可靠性。

Method: 基于大规模儿童队列数据集（n>1000），采用成分数据分析（CoDA）构建目标函数，捕捉日常活动构成与多重健康指标（BMI、生活满意度、认知等）之间的关系；提出一种将预测不确定性融入质量多样性（QD）过程的新方法，并通过基于变量和基于目标的行为表征探索解空间，揭示不确定性条件下多样化高质量时间使用构成及健康结果间的显式关系。

Result: 该框架成功将时间使用推荐引导至低不确定性区域，同时保留高质量的解结构；通过QD探索，揭示了不同时间分配方案在多重健康结果之间的权衡关系，并生成了兼顾预期收益与置信度的多样化可靠推荐方案。

Conclusion: 将不确定性直接嵌入优化过程，可以在保持高质量时间使用结构的同时，将推荐方案转向更可靠的低不确定性区域，为行为健康领域提供更稳健的决策支持。该框架为健康时间使用推荐设立了新的可靠性标准。

Abstract: The daily allocation of the finite 24-hour time budget is strongly associated with physical, mental, and cognitive health. While predictive models can estimate the relationship between time-use compositions and health outcomes such as body mass index, life satisfaction, and cognition, most optimization approaches focus only on maximizing expected benefit and do not consider the uncertainty inherent in data-driven prediction. Ignoring uncertainty in health-related decisions can lead to unrealistic time-use recommendations. To address this gap, we introduce an uncertainty quantification Quality Diversity (QD) framework for a more reliable time-use recommendation. Objective functions are derived using compositional data analysis using a large child cohort dataset n > 1000, to capture the relationship between daily activity compositions and multiple health indicators. We develop a new approach that incorporates predictive uncertainty into QD processes and produces more reliable recommendations that balance the expected health benefits with the confidence of the model. We explore the solution space through variable-based and objective-based behavioral representations, revealing diverse high-quality time-use composition and explicit relationships between health outcomes under uncertainty. By embedding uncertainty directly into optimization, our framework shifts the time-use recommendations toward regions of lower uncertainty while preserving high-quality structures for more reliable decision-making in behavioral health.

</details>


### [259] [A Unified Causal Inference Framework for the Desirability of Outcome Ranking Paradigm in Benefit-Risk Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05244)
*Yuan Feng,Shiyu Shu,Yixin Fang,Ionut Bebu,Toshimitsu Hamasaki,Scott Evans,Guoqing Diao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的协变量调整因果推断框架，用于估计期望结局排序（DOOR）概率以进行获益-风险评价，并通过模拟研究系统比较了G-computation、IPW、AIPW和TMLE等多种估计方法的性能，最终推荐CVTMLE-SL作为最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 获益-风险评价在随机试验和观察性研究中至关重要，而DOOR概率作为一种将多个有序结局整合为单一指标的方法，其因果推断缺乏系统且统一的协变量调整估计框架。现有方法在估计效率、稳健性及统计推断方面尚未得到充分比较和评估。

Method: 将DOOR概率表达为两种治疗策略下边际有序结局分布的双线性泛函，通过序列风险集风险模型估计条件有序分布，并推导出DOOR概率的有效影响函数（EIF）。在点估计模拟中比较了G-computation、归一化IPW、AIPW和TMLE，每种方法的冗余函数分别用广义线性模型和Super Learner估计。随后在多种设置下（不同重叠程度、治疗效果异质性、治疗分配比例）评估了AIPW-SL和TMLE-SL基于EIF的推断性能，并引入交叉拟合。

Result: 点估计方面，TMLE-SL表现最强且最稳定，AIPW-SL排名第二。在推断性能方面，CVTMLE-SL在DOOR尺度偏倚、底层有序分布恢复、标准误准确性和置信区间覆盖率等指标上整体表现最优。

Conclusion: 基于EIF的方法（尤其是CVTMLE-SL）为DOOR概率的协变量调整估计和推断提供了统一且稳健的框架，适用于随机试验和观察性研究中的获益-风险评价。

Abstract: We developed a unified covariate-adjusted causal inference framework for estimating the desirability of outcome ranking (DOOR) probability for benefit-risk evaluation in randomized trials and observational studies. The framework expresses the DOOR probability as a bilinear functional of the marginal ordinal outcome distributions under the two treatment strategies, estimates conditional ordinal distributions through sequential risk-set hazards, and derives the efficient influence function (EIF) of the DOOR probability. The point-estimation simulations compared G-computation, normalized inverse probability weighting (IPW), augmented IPW (AIPW), and targeted maximum likelihood estimation (TMLE), with nuisance functions estimated using generalized linear models or Super Learner (SL). TMLE-SL showed the strongest and most consistent point-estimation performance, with AIPW-SL ranking second. EIF-based inference was then evaluated for AIPW-SL and TMLE-SL, with and without cross-fitting, across settings varying in overlap, treatment-effect heterogeneity, and treatment allocation. CVTMLE-SL showed the strongest overall performance across DOOR-scale bias, recovery of the underlying ordinal distributions, standard-error accuracy, and confidence-interval coverage. We illustrate the methodology using data from the multidrug-resistant organism network of the Antibacterial Resistance Leadership Group.

</details>


### [260] [Deep Generalised Mixed Models: a Novel Neural Network Structure for Analysing Hierarchical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05930)
*Nina van Gerwen,Dimitris Rizopoulos,Manon Hillegers,Loes Keijsers,Sten Willemsen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的深度广义混合模型（Deep Generalised Mixed Model），通过结合变分自编码器和贝叶斯数据增强算法，解决经验抽样法（ESM）纵向数据中因随机缺失（MAR）导致的偏差问题，并在GrowIt!青少年情绪研究中进行了应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机源于GrowIt!应用在COVID-19期间收集的青少年日常情绪ESM数据。传统统计方法难以扩展到高维场景，而标准机器学习方法因数据缺失（青少年因先前强烈负面情绪而退出，导致随机缺失数据）而产生选择偏差，无法提供有效推断。

Method: 提出深度广义混合模型，将混合效应模型推广到深度学习框架。该模型通过固定效应和随机效应对数据的均值和相关结构进行半参数灵活建模。参数估计采用变分自编码器的改进版本与贝叶斯数据增强算法相结合，可处理任意分布的纵向结局变量。

Result: 在GrowIt!研究及多项模拟实验中，深度广义混合模型展现出潜力，能够适应高维数据并在随机缺失机制下提供有效推断，但存在因模型不稳定性导致的次优表现。

Conclusion: 深度广义混合模型为ESM数据分析提供了一种有前景的解决方案，能够克服缺失数据和高维挑战，但模型稳定性仍需进一步改进。

Abstract: The experience sampling method (ESM) is a longitudinal research design where participants report their thoughts, emotional states and behaviours multiple times a day. Our work is motivated by such data collected by the GrowIt! app, which was released to investigate daily emotions among adolescents during the COVID-19 pandemic. Current procedures to analyse ESM data face various challenges. While standard statistical techniques may not scale well to a high-dimensional setting, machine learning procedures can give biased results due to selection bias introduced by missingness. In our motivating dataset, adolescents dropped out due to previous strong feelings of negative emotions. Hence, the implied missing data are of the missing-at-random type that standard machine learning procedures cannot accommodate. We develop a novel neural network architecture that generalises mixed effects models to deep learning to overcome these challenges. It allows semi-parametric and flexible modelling of data's mean and correlation structure through fixed and random effects. For estimation, we use an adaptation of variational auto-encoders and a Bayesian data augmentation algorithm. Through this approach, the model can accommodate longitudinal outcomes following generic distributions, scale well to high-dimensional settings and provide valid inference when data are missing-at-random. We applied the Deep Generalised Mixed Model to the GrowIt! study and various simulations. The results show potential for the Deep Generalised Mixed Model, yet suboptimal performance due to model instability.

</details>


### [261] [Handling Missing Data in Probabilistic Regression Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06195)
*Taiane Schaedler Prass,Alisson Silva Neimaier,Guilherme Pumi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: PRTrees被扩展以在树构建过程中直接处理缺失预测变量，提出三种策略（均匀概率、部分观测、降维平滑），无需事先插补。实验表明，缺失值处理策略对预测性能的影响甚至超过平滑分布和代理选择准则，在高缺失率数据集上常优于CART。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的概率回归树（PRTrees）虽具有平滑性和一致性优势，但无法直接处理缺失预测变量，实践中必须先进行插补。作者旨在消除这一预处理步骤，使PRTree在树构建阶段就能原生地适应缺失数据，从而避免插补引入的偏差和信息损失。

Method: 在PRTree框架内提出三种缺失值处理策略：(1) 均匀概率法——对缺失协变量赋予均匀的拆分分配概率；(2) 部分观测法——仅基于观测到的维度计算概率，保持可用信息的原始结构；(3) 降维平滑法——在降维空间中进行概率平滑。三种策略均被证明在任意缺失模式下保持概率守恒和边际相容性。在多个真实数据集上，系统比较了填充策略、平滑分布和代理选择准则三者对性能的相对贡献。

Result: 填充策略（fill strategy）成为主导建模组件，对预测性能的影响往往超过平滑分布或代理选择准则。在预测变量缺失比例较高的数据集上，所提方法常优于CART，同时保持了树模型的可解释性和灵活性。

Conclusion: PRTree框架可有效扩展以原生支持缺失数据，三种策略各有信息利用方式。缺失值处理方式的选择对概率树构建效果至关重要，在某些场景下甚至比树结构本身的设计选择更具影响力。该方法在保持树模型优势的同时，在高缺失率场景中展现出优于传统CART的预测性能。

Abstract: Probabilistic Regression Trees (PRTrees) are a smooth and consistent alternative to classical regression trees, producing continuous predictions through probabilistic split assignments. This paper extends the PRTree framework to accommodate missing predictor values directly during tree construction, eliminating the need for prior imputation. Three strategies are proposed, each exploiting the available information differently: a uniform-probability approach, a partial-observation approach, and a dimension-reduced smoothing approach. These modifications are defined to preserve the fundamental probabilistic properties of the original methodology, including probability conservation and marginal compatibility, under arbitrary patterns of missing covariate values. The proposed methods are evaluated on several real-world datasets exhibiting different levels of missingness and are compared with classical regression trees. The results show that the effectiveness of probabilistic tree construction depends strongly on the treatment of missing observations. Across the considered datasets, the fill strategy emerged as the dominant modeling component, often exerting a larger influence on predictive performance than either the smoothing distribution or the proxy-selection criterion. In datasets where a substantial proportion of observations contained missing predictor values, the proposed methods frequently outperformed CART, while maintaining the interpretability and flexibility of tree-based models.

</details>


### [262] [Beyond Marginal Validity: Finite-Sample Guarantees for Localized Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06206)
*Anton Conrad,Rustam Isaev,Denis Belomestny,Eric Moulines,Sergey Samsonov*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文为随机局部共形预测（RLCP）提供了首个有限样本下联合控制条件有效性和预言效率的理论保证，在高阶光滑性假设下证明了条件覆盖间隙和长度误差的高概率一致界，并将结果推广到数据分割学习评分的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测仅保证边际覆盖，可能掩盖协变量特异性的误校准；精确的条件覆盖在有限样本下不可实现。RLCP通过在测试点附近校准来缓解此问题，但现有理论缺乏对其局部集合的条件有效性和预言效率的有限样本保证。

Method: 固定评分情形下，假设条件评分CDF满足Hölder正则性及标准密度和核假设，证明在实现局部邻域上一致的高概率界；进一步将分析推广到数据分割学习的评分（如共形分位数回归），将局部一致界分解为固定评分校准误差和均匀评分估计误差之和。

Result: 条件覆盖间隙和相对预言的长度误差的界可分解为O(h^β)局部化偏差和随校准样本量递减的校准项，阐明了带宽偏差-方差权衡以及RLCP追踪预言的条件；在数据分割学习评分下，局部一致保证分解为固定评分校准和均匀评分估计误差，表明评分学习质量的提升可锐化局部保证。

Conclusion: 本文首次为RLCP提供了完整的有限样本理论分析，明确了在何种条件下RLCP能够同时逼近条件覆盖和预言效率，并揭示了评分学习质量对局部保证的直接影响，为实际应用中的超参数选择提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Conformal prediction endows arbitrary black-box predictors with finite-sample, distribution-free marginal coverage, yet marginal validity can hide severe covariate-specific miscalibration, while exact distribution-free conditional coverage is finite-sample unattainable. Randomly localized conformal prediction (RLCP) mitigates this gap by calibrating near the test point while preserving marginal coverage. Existing theory, however, lacks finite-sample guarantees for the realized localized set that jointly control conditional validity and oracle efficiency. We provide such guarantees. For any fixed score, under Hölder regularity of the conditional score CDF and standard density and kernel assumptions, we prove high-probability bounds, uniform over a realized localization neighbourhood, for the conditional-coverage gap and the length error relative to the oracle. The bounds decompose into an $O(h^β)$ localization bias and a calibration term decreasing with calibration size, clarifying the bandwidth bias-variance tradeoff and when RLCP tracks the oracle. We also analyze data-split learned scores: when the score targets a pivotal score, as in conformalized quantile regression, uniform local guarantees decompose into fixed-score calibration and uniform score-estimation errors, showing that improved learning sharpens localized guarantees.

</details>


### [263] [Minimax Optimal Early-Stopped Gradient Descent for Gaussian Mixture Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06250)
*Alex Buna,Shirley Xiaoqi Liu,Patrick Rebeschini*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 在过参数化分类中，梯度下降于逻辑损失下会收敛到最大间隔插值分类器，但其隐式偏差可能统计次优。本文证明，在高斯混合模型带标签翻转噪声的场景下，经适当oracle时间早停的梯度下降可在协方差谱快速衰减时达到极小极大最优的零一风险，并提供了校准结果将逻辑风险转化为零一风险，同时证明插值分类器可能需要指数级更多样本才能达到相同风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化分类中，即使数据分布线性不可分，训练数据也可被线性分离。梯度下降在逻辑损失下发散但方向收敛到最大间隔插值分类器，其隐式偏差在统计上可能是次优的。作者旨在探索早停法能否克服这种次优性，达到统计最优的分类性能。

Method: 在带标签翻转噪声的高斯混合模型框架下，分析梯度下降早停迭代的性质。核心方法包括：(1) 对早停迭代建立锐利上界；(2) 对任意分类器建立匹配的统计下界；(3) 提出一种新的校准结果，将超额逻辑风险转化为超额零一风险，能处理标签翻转噪声引起的模型误设，并移除了标准界中的平方根速率；(4) 建立线性插值器的下界，展示早停相对于插值的样本效率优势。

Result: 早停在合适oracle时间停止可在多项式衰减和指数衰减等快速连续衰减的协方差谱上达到极小极大最优的超额零一风险。实验验证了理论速率的正确性。线性插值器需指数级更多样本才能达到与早停相同的超额风险，说明早停具有显著的样本效率优势。

Conclusion: 早停是一种简单有效的策略，能够克服过参数化分类中最大间隔插值的统计次优性。新的校准不等式是关键技术贡献，移除了标准校准界中的平方根衰减，使分析更紧致。早停在样本复杂度上远优于插值分类器。

Abstract: In overparameterised classification, training data can be linearly separable even when the underlying distribution is not. In this setting, gradient descent (GD) on the logistic loss diverges in norm while converging in direction to a max-margin interpolating classifier, whose implicit bias can be statistically suboptimal. In this work, we show that early stopping can overcome this suboptimality: in a Gaussian mixture model with label-flipping noise, GD stopped at an appropriate oracle time achieves minimax-optimal excess zero-one risk for covariance spectra with fast and continuous decay, including polynomial and exponential spectral decays. Our analysis combines a sharp upper bound for the early-stopped iterate with a matching statistical lower bound over arbitrary classifiers, yielding optimal rates that are validated by experiments. A central technical contribution is a new calibration result that converts excess logistic risk into excess zero-one risk; it handles the model misspecification induced by the label-flipping noise, and removes the square-root rate in standard bounds. We also establish a lower bound for linear interpolators, showing that interpolation can require exponentially more samples than early stopping to achieve the same excess risk.

</details>


### [264] [Stochastic Dynamics on Persistence Diagram Space via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06276)
*Farzana Nasrin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文引入强化学习框架，在持久性图（PD）空间上定义随机动力学，通过拓扑感知的局部编辑操作使图演化。文章证明了诱导马尔可夫链的不可约性、非周期性和几何遍历性，从而保证唯一平稳分布的存在。实验表明该方法能在降低图复杂度的同时保留主导拓扑结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PD统计分析文献多将图视为静态对象，缺乏对PD空间上概率建模和随机演化的框架。需要一种能在PD空间上进行受控随机动力学建模的方法，既能实现概率建模，又能引导图向科学相关的拓扑目标演化。

Method: 提出强化学习框架，定义PD空间上的受控马尔可夫过程：通过拓扑感知的局部编辑操作（如增删点）使图演化，支持可变基数。设计奖励函数兼顾分布匹配、任务特定拓扑统计量和结构保持压缩。证明诱导链的不可约性、非周期性和几何遍历性，保证唯一平稳分布存在。

Result: 理论上证明了满足一定条件时，诱导马尔可夫链具有不可约性、非周期性和几何遍历性，从而在PD空间上存在唯一平稳概率律。合成数据和神经影像PD实验表明，框架能在显著降低图复杂度的同时，保留主导拓扑结构。

Conclusion: 所提框架为PD空间上的自适应拓扑简化和概率建模提供了统一方法，填补了PD随机动力学建模的空白，在拓扑数据分析领域具有理论和应用双重价值。

Abstract: Persistence diagrams (PDs) provide stable and interpretable summaries of multiscale topological structure. While substantial progress has been made in the statistical analysis of PDs, existing literature often treats diagrams as static objects and provide limited frameworks for probabilistic modeling and stochastic evolution on PD space. We introduce a reinforcement learning framework for stochastic dynamics on PD space, where diagrams evolve through topology aware local edit operations. The dynamics define controlled Markov processes on spaces of finite PDs with variable cardinality. We establish conditions under which the induced Markov chains are irreducible, aperiodic, and geometrically ergodic, implying the existence of unique stationary probability laws on PD space. To guide the dynamics toward scientifically relevant topological targets, we formulate objectives that encompass distribution matching, task specific topological statistics, and structure-preserving compression. The resulting rewards balance task specific distributional targets, diagram fidelity, and complexity reduction, and yield a framework for adaptive topological simplification and probabilistic modeling. Experiments on synthetic and neuroimaging PDs demonstrate that the proposed framework can preserve dominant topological structure while reducing diagram complexity.

</details>


### [265] [Optimal Rates for Learning with Monotone Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06337)
*Anay Mehrotra*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种由Larsen等人提出的对抗性插入模型：敌手观察i.i.d.干净样本后插入有限个正确标记的额外样本，学习者看到洗牌后的合并样本。作者证明，除VC维为1的情况外，额外对数因子是固有的——极小极大误差在d=1时为Θ(1/n)，d≥2时为Θ((d/n)log(n/d))。该下界同样适用于Littlestone维度，表明即使对在线学习中有有限错误界的类，添加正确标记样本反会使学习难度增加对数因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Larsen等人提出的模型中，经验风险最小化(ERM)达到O((d/n)log(n/d))的误差，而已知PAC学习中最优速率为Θ(d/n)。核心问题：这个额外对数因子是特定算法的缺陷，还是缺失可交换性带来的固有代价？

Method: 上界：对d=1，设计了一种简单的非真(improper)学习器，通过改编一包含图(one-inclusion graph)中的留一法(leave-one-out)论证实现Θ(1/n)速率。下界：对所有d≥2，构造一个显式的假设类和先验，使得两个仅在不可忽略质量的点上不同的目标假设产生完全相同的样本分布，从而导出Θ((d/n)log(n/d))的下界。

Result: 极小极大期望误差在VC维d=1时为Θ(1/n)，d≥2时为Θ((d/n)log(n/d))。相同速率对Littlestone维度d_L成立，表明干净的在线到批处理速率O(d_L/n)不可达。

Conclusion: 相当反直觉的结论：添加正确标记的样本反而会使学习难度增加一个对数因子，即使在在线学习中具有有限错误界的类也不例外。额外对数代价是缺失可交换性的固有结果，而非特定算法的局限。

Abstract: A monotone adversary observes an i.i.d. labeled sample and appends a finite number of further examples of its choice, every one of them labeled correctly by the target hypothesis. The learner sees a uniform shuffle of the combined sample and is scored on the original distribution. Every example is correctly labeled, but the insertions depend on the clean sample, so the combined sample is not exchangeable. Larsen, Pabbaraju, and Shetty, who introduced this model, showed that empirical risk minimization attains expected error $O((d/n)\log(n/d))$ for classes of VC dimension $d$, and that every known optimal learner can be pushed away from the $Θ(d/n)$ rate, optimal for PAC learning. They asked whether the extra logarithm is an artifact of those particular algorithms or an inherent consequence of the lack of exchangeability.
  We show that this additional cost is inherent beyond VC dimension one. In the worst case over classes of VC dimension $d$ and over known finite insertion budgets, the minimax expected error is $Θ(1/n)$ at $d=1$ and $Θ((d/n)\log(n/d))$ for $d\geq 2$. The same rates hold with Littlestone dimension $d_{\mathrm L}$ in place of $d$, so the clean online-to-batch rate $O(d_{\mathrm L}/n)$ is unattainable as well. Thus, somewhat counterintuitively, adding correctly labeled examples can make learning harder by a logarithmic factor, even for classes that admit finite mistake bounds in online learning. The dimension-one upper bound is achieved by a simple improper learner whose analysis adapts the leave-one-out argument underlying the one-inclusion graph. All of our lower bounds are elementary and come from a single construction: an explicit class and prior on which two target hypothesis, which differ a point of nonnegligible mass, produce the same sample.

</details>


### [266] [Scalable estimation of VARMA models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06340)
*Daniel Paulin,Victor Elvira*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种VARMA模型的高效估计框架，通过偏自相关重参数化、高斯先验和Parseval恒等式，使每次优化迭代的计算成本与序列长度T无关，从而将基于似然的VARMA估计扩展到此前只能使用VAR模型的中等维度问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VARMA模型的MA项能用少量参数捕捉纯AR需多阶滞后才能匹配的动态结构，但传统估计受限于三大障碍：似然非凸、参数仅可识别到等价类、每次评估需遍历整个序列。这使得VARMA在中等维度以上被认为不实用，实践者被迫依赖VAR模型。

Method: 框架包含三个核心组件：(1)偏自相关重参数化，从构造上保证平稳性和可逆性；(2)高斯先验置于重参数化系数上，并对对角与非对角元素采用不同尺度；(3)基于Parseval（傅里叶）恒等式的损失函数，将数据依赖压缩为固定大小的充分统计量，截断长度下计算成本近线性。由此得到两种点估计：正则化最小二乘拟合与协方差边际化的最大后验估计。框架还扩展至季节性动态、外生回归变量(VA RMX)及滚动窗口重拟合。

Result: 理论上证明两种估计量在固定维度下以近参数速率恢复真实过程的无限自回归表示，截断不引入渐近偏差。实证上，从d=10到d=40，估计量的预测误差接近Oracle水平（而经典条件MLE给出不可逆拟合导致预测发散），在零售需求、气象和空气质量数据上匹配或优于VAR、BVAR、分量ARMA和稀疏VARMA基线。

Conclusion: 该框架以与序列长度无关的单次迭代成本，将基于似然的VARMA估计带入了此前实践者只能依赖VAR模型的问题规模，消除了VARMA在实际应用中的计算障碍。

Abstract: Vector autoregressive moving-average (VARMA) models have long been considered impractical beyond moderate dimensions: the likelihood is non-convex, the parametrization is identified only up to equivalence, and every evaluation costs a pass over the entire series. Yet their moving-average term captures with a few parameters what a pure autoregression matches only with many lags. We introduce an estimation framework that removes this computational barrier: each optimization iteration is independent of the series length $T$. The framework combines a partial-autocorrelation reparametrization that guarantees stationarity and invertibility by construction, Gaussian priors on the reparametrized coefficients with separate scales for diagonal and off-diagonal entries, and losses that depend on the data only through fixed-size sufficient statistics, evaluated by a Parseval (Fourier) identity at near-linear cost in the truncation length. This yields two point estimators: a regularized least-squares fit and a covariance-marginalized maximum-a-posteriori estimator. We prove that both recover the infinite-autoregressive representation of the true process at a near-parametric rate in fixed dimension, so the truncation introduces no asymptotic bias. The same machinery extends, at the same leading cost, to seasonal dynamics, exogenous regressors (VARMAX), and rolling-window refits. Empirically, the estimators stay close to the oracle forecast error from $d=10$ to $d=40$ (where classical conditional MLE returns non-invertible fits whose forecasts diverge) and match or beat VAR, Bayesian-VAR, component-wise ARMA, and sparse-VARMA baselines on retail-demand, meteorological, and air-quality data. This brings likelihood-based VARMA estimation, at a per-iteration cost independent of the series length, to the problem sizes where practitioners have so far relied on VAR models.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

### [267] [Agentic Nesting: A New Methodology for Existing Enterprise Application Integration and Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05159)
*Xi Wang,Kun Li,Xianyao Ling,Gang Yin,Liang Zhang,Jiang Wu,Wenbo Lei,Jun Xu,Annie Wang,Fu Zhang,Weizhe Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Nesting框架，将企业遗留应用封装为层级嵌套的自主AI代理，通过"应用即代理"范式与"对话即集成"理念，解决企业异构系统间的数据孤岛与流程碎片化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业面临严重的异构系统数据孤岛与流程碎片化问题。传统的企业应用集成方案（ESB、API网关、RPA）存在架构耦合度高、运维成本攀升及智能化能力有限等固有缺陷，难以有效编排和利用已有应用。

Method: 提出Agentic Nesting多智能体协作框架，核心包括：(1) 将现有企业应用封装为自主AI代理，构建层级嵌套的管家拓扑结构以映射企业生态系统的组合复杂性；(2) 从每个遗留应用提取数字代理（Digital Agent Proxy），赋予其自然语言交互与自主操控能力；(3) 通过中央编排器协调多个代理，实现任务分解与动态调度；(4) 暴露统一对话接口，支持跨应用查询与流程编排。

Result: 论文主要贡献包括：(1) 提出"Application-as-Agent"（应用即代理）集成范式；(2) 提出"Conversation-as-Integration"（对话即集成）交互哲学；(3) 探索该方法在异构系统协调与大规模数据应用场景中的泛化潜力。

Conclusion: Agentic Nesting框架通过将应用抽象为可对话的AI代理并以层级嵌套方式组织，提供了一种突破传统集成方案局限的新路径，有望在异构系统协调和大规模数据应用等场景中实现更灵活、智能的企业应用集成。

Abstract: Enterprise operations extensively rely on multiple heterogeneous business systems and information applications, which also result in severe data silos and process fragmentation. Enterprises have invested considerable financial and material resources in building these applications, however, effectively leveraging and orchestrating them remains a formidable challenge. Conventional approaches to enterprise application integration, encompassing middleware architectures such as Enterprise Service Bus (ESB), API gateway infrastructures, and Robotic Process Automation (RPA), suffer from inherent limitations like high architectural coupling, escalating operation and maintenance costs, and limited intelligence capabilities. This paper proposes Agentic Nesting, a multi-agent collaboration framework in which existing enterprise applications are encapsulated as autonomous AI agents within a hierarchically nested structure. Rather than flat interconnection, agents are organized into layered stewardship topologies that mirror the compositional complexity of enterprise ecosystems. The framework extracts a digital agent proxy from each legacy application to enable natural-language interaction and autonomous manipulation, coordinates multiple agents through a central orchestrator for task decomposition and dynamic dispatching, and exposes a unified conversational interface for cross-application querying and process orchestration. The main contributions of this paper are the proposition of the "Application-as-Agent" integration paradigm and the "Conversation-as-Integration" interaction philosophy, together with an exploration of the generalization potential of this methodology in scenarios encompassing heterogeneous system coordination, and large-scale data applications.

</details>


### [268] [The Ignition Index: Measuring Global Workspace Dynamics in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05160)
*Saman Rahbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Ignition Index（I），一个基于全局工作空间理论（GWT）的标量度量，通过拟合四参数S型函数到逐层线性探针精度曲线，提取陡度参数β̂来量化Transformer语言模型中的"点火"（ignition）现象。在11个模型、5种架构族上的实验表明，该指标对真实语言结构的选择性比随机标签对照高9.6倍，能够有效区分前馈Transformer与状态空间模型（SSM）、揭示循环架构沿迭代轴的点火行为，并检测到训练过程中的相变点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局工作空间理论（GWT）提出了"全或无"点火（all-or-none ignition）的认知预测，但缺乏将该理论操作化为可量化度量的方法，尤其在Transformer语言模型的机制可解释性领域。作者旨在构建首个能够桥接GWT动力学预测与深度学习机制分析的定量工具。

Method: Ignition Index通过以下步骤构建：（1）对模型每层训练线性探针，测量其在不同输入信号强度下的表征能力；（2）将探针精度作为信号强度的函数，拟合四参数S型曲线；（3）提取陡度参数β̂作为核心度量——高β̂表示突变的、点火式过渡，低β̂表示渐进累积。实验涵盖11个模型、5种架构族，使用随机标签打乱作为对照，以Mann-Whitney U检验验证选择性。此外，采用PELT（Pruned Exact Linear Time）算法检测训练过程中的相变点。

Result: 四项核心发现：（1）前馈Transformer的聚合β̂比SSM（如Mamba）高89%（p<1e-13, Cohen's d=0.52），Mamba呈现近乎线性的过渡曲线，表明缺乏全局广播机制；（2）Huginn-3.5B沿迭代轴的点火强度是其深度轴的2.12倍，证明循环架构沿循环维度表现出工作空间式过渡；（3）Pythia-410M在训练步256处出现PELT检测到的相变（+67%），早于诱导头（induction head）的形成；（4）关于模型规模和信号强度与点火相关的假设未被证实，提示Transformer架构可能已饱和其可用点火机制。随机标签对照显示该指标对真实语言结构的选择性为9.6倍。

Conclusion: Ignition Index提供了首个经过验证的GWT动力学预测与机制可解释性之间的定量桥梁，具备9.6倍的测量选择性和架构级别的区分能力，这些特性在此前的scaling文献中未被表征。该指标为理解语言模型中的全局信息整合与广播机制提供了新的分析维度。

Abstract: We introduce the Ignition Index (I), a validated scalar metric that operationalizes Global Workspace Theory's (GWT) all-or-none ignition prediction in transformer language models. The metric fits a four-parameter sigmoid to per-layer linear probe accuracy as a function of input signal strength, extracting steepness parameter beta-hat: high values indicate abrupt, ignition-like transitions; low values indicate graded build-up. Across 11 models spanning five architecture families, shuffled-label controls demonstrate 9.6-fold selectivity for genuine linguistic structure over spurious probe capacity (p < 0.001, Mann-Whitney U-test). We find: (1) Feedforward transformers exceed SSMs by 89% in aggregate beta-hat (p < 1e-13, Cohen's d = 0.52), with Mamba exhibiting near-linear profiles consistent with absent global broadcast. (2) Huginn-3.5B exhibits 2.12-fold higher ignition along its iteration axis than its depth axis, demonstrating that recurrent architectures manifest workspace-like transitions along the recurrence dimension. (3) Pythia-410M shows a PELT-detected phase transition at training step 256 (+67%), preceding induction-head formation. (4) Hypotheses linking ignition to model scale and signal strength were not confirmed, suggesting transformer architectures may saturate available ignition mechanisms. The Ignition Index provides the first validated quantitative bridge between GWT's dynamical predictions and mechanistic interpretability, with 9.6-fold measurement selectivity and architecture-level discriminability not previously characterized in the scaling literature. Code: https://github.com/saman-rahbar/ignition-index

</details>


### [269] [Woodpecker Distillation: Weak Models Diagnose Reasoning Bugs in Strong Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05168)
*Dayu Wang,Jiaye Yang,Weikang Li,Jiahui Liang,Yang Li,Deguo Xia,Jizhou Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大语言模型在推理任务上的失败常源于中间步骤的局部推理错误，而非全局能力不足。本文发现这些错误可通过弱模型生成的短修补块修复，但直接微调无法可靠内化此信号。为此提出 Woodpecker Distillation，一种通过对比局部干预进行弱到强训练的框架，在数学推理基准上持续提升强模型性能并优于直接模仿基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽然具备解决推理任务的能力，却经常失败。作者认为失败源于中间步骤的局部推理错误（localized reasoning bugs），这些错误是可修复的——弱探针模型生成的短修补块能重定向推理轨迹至正确解。但直接微调无法可靠内化这种修复信号，因为有用信号不在于干预文本本身，而在于其如何重塑模型未来的推理分布。

Method: 提出 Woodpecker Distillation（啄木鸟蒸馏），一种弱到强训练框架。核心思路是：在同一推理前缀下对比成功与失败的弱模型修补块，利用它们各自诱导的未来 token 预测分布构建一个修正性教师分布（corrective teacher distribution），然后将该信号蒸馏到强模型中，从而让强模型学会自我修正推理过程。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的实验表明，Woodpecker Distillation 能够持续提升强模型的推理性能，且优于直接模仿弱修补块或修复轨迹的基线方法。

Conclusion: 局部推理错误可被弱模型修补，但有效信号在于对推理分布的重塑而非修补文本本身。Woodpecker Distillation 通过对比局部干预学习修正性分布，为弱到强训练提供了更有效的范式。

Abstract: Large language models often fail on reasoning tasks despite possessing the capability to solve them. We argue that many such failures arise from localized reasoning bugs in intermediate steps rather than from global incompetence. We show that these bugs are frequently repairable: inserting a short patch generated by a weak probe model after the same strong-model reasoning prefix can redirect the trajectory toward a correct solution.
  However, this corrective effect is not reliably internalized by directly fine-tuning on weak patches or repaired trajectories, suggesting that the useful signal lies not in the intervention text itself, but in how it reshapes the model's future reasoning distribution. We therefore propose Woodpecker Distillation, a weak-to-strong training framework that learns from contrastive local interventions. Our method contrasts successful and unsuccessful weak-model patches at the same prefix, constructs a corrective teacher distribution from their induced future token predictions, and distills this signal into the strong model.
  Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that Woodpecker Distillation consistently improves strong-model performance and outperforms direct imitation baselines.

</details>


### [270] [From Continuous Predictors to Clinical Thresholds: Early Evidence on Performance Trade-offs of Guideline-Based Categorisation for Ischaemic Stroke Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05203)
*Esra Zihni,Katryna Cisek,Hamzah Ziadeh,Hendrik Knoche,Robert Mikulik,John D. Kelleher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨在急性缺血性卒中90天结局预测中，用符合临床指南的离散化类别变量替代连续变量，结果显示在大多数治疗队列中分类模型与连续模型预测性能统计无差异，且全局特征重要性排序保持一致，表明基于指南的分类编码是可行的设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管机器学习模型在卒中结局预测中精度很高，但模型解释与临床医生的推理方式不匹配，导致临床采纳受限。受一项临床用户研究的启发，该研究呼吁采用与临床指南一致的截断值，作者提出用临床信息驱动的分类编码替代连续预测变量。

Method: 基于欧洲多中心注册数据，按三种治疗队列分层，分别构建标准梯度提升模型（使用连续变量）和完全分类化梯度提升模型（使用符合卒中指南的、治疗特异性的阈值），比较两者的预测性能与特征重要性排序。

Result: 在三个治疗队列中的两个，完全分类化模型与连续变量模型的预测性能统计无显著差异，仅在一个队列中出现显著下降。全局特征重要性排名在各治疗组间保持一致。

Conclusion: 将连续预测变量按指南阈值离散化为分类变量，在保留预后因素核心层次结构的同时，是一种可行的卒中结局模型设计选择，有助于弥合模型解释与临床推理之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Machine learning models achieve strong predictive accuracy for 90-day outcome prediction in acute ischaemic stroke, yet clinical adoption is limited by the misalignment of model explanations with clinicians' reasoning. Motivated by a clinician user study calling for clinical guideline-aligned cut-offs, we ask whether continuous predictors can be replaced by clinically informed categorical encodings without sacrificing performance. On a multi-centre European registry stratified into three treatment cohorts, we compare standard and fully categorised gradient-boosted models, the latter using stroke guideline-aligned, treatment-specific thresholds. The fully categorised models are statistically indistinguishable from their continuous counterparts in two of the treatment cohorts, with a significant drop in predictive accuracy in one cohort. Global feature importance rankings remain consistent, suggesting that discretising continuous predictors into guideline-based categories preserves the core hierarchy of prognostic factors across all treatment groups. Guideline-based categorisation is thus a viable design choice for stroke-outcome models.

</details>


### [271] [SkillTrace: Multi-Trace Provenance Auditing for LLM-Agent Skill Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05204)
*Jialuo Chen,Minghe Wang,Lingqi Jiang,Jianan Ma,Xinhao Deng,Xiaohu Du,Ruixiao Lin,Yunhao Feng,Linkang Du,Jingyi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SKILLTRACE 是一个面向 LLM-agent 技能复用的多溯源审计框架，通过提取表达、实现和操作三条溯源轨迹，构建技能操作图（SOG），在校验时进行确定性比较，在基准测试上取得 AUROC 0.938 和 F1 0.898，并在大规模实际审计中有效发现可操作的复用审查队列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-agent 生态系统中的可复用技能（skills）不仅是代码，而是包含元数据、自然语言指令、工具引用和工作流的多模态制品。现有的克隆检测方法仅针对单模态源代码或整体包相似度，无法捕捉仅保留技能某一局部（如文本描述或操作结构）的复用行为，因此需要一种能跨模态追踪复用证据的新审计方法。

Method: 提出 SKILLTRACE 框架，提取三类溯源轨迹：(1) Expression Trace——作者文本层面的证据；(2) Implementation Trace——代码片段层面的证据；(3) Operational Trace——以技能操作图（SOG）形式刻画激活、流程和资源流动结构。其中仅操作轨迹的提取借助 LLM 在摄入阶段一次性完成；审计时对已缓存的轨迹进行确定性比对，并利用同功能的严格负例对各轨迹进行校准，最终报告支持复用判定的具体轨迹。

Result: 在 SKILLTRACE-BENCH 上，包含 100 个市场锚点技能的 820 个变换后正例和 751 个负例对照，框架取得 AUROC 0.938、F1 0.898。在 36,446 个技能的大规模实际审计中，基于轨迹归因的证据能有效浮现超越仓库级基线的可操作复用审查队列。

Conclusion: SKILLTRACE 通过多溯源轨迹审计有效解决了 LLM-agent 技能复用检测中跨模态、局部复用的难题，其确定性比对和轨迹级校准机制兼具高精度与可解释性，适用于大规模技能市场的实际部署。

Abstract: LLM-agent ecosystems are rapidly growing around reusable skills: mixed-modality packages of metadata, natural-language instructions, code, tools, references, and operational workflows. As skills become marketplace artifacts, auditing their reuse is no longer the same problem as ordinary code clone detection. Existing detectors target single-modality source code or whole-package similarity, yet skill reuse evidence is distributed across authored text, implementation fragments, and operational structure. As a result, they can miss reuse that preserves only one part of a skill. We present SKILLTRACE, a multi-trace provenance auditing framework for LLM-agent skill reuse. SKILLTRACE extracts three provenance traces: Expression, Implementation, and Operational. It represents the Operational Trace as a Skill Operational Graph (SOG) that captures activation, procedure, and resource-flow structure. An LLM assists only the Operational-trace extraction, once at ingestion; at audit time SKILLTRACE compares cached traces deterministically, calibrates each trace against same-function strict negatives, and reports which trace supports a reuse decision. On SKILLTRACE-BENCH, with 820 transformed reuse positives over 100 marketplace anchors and 751 negative controls, SKILLTRACE achieves AUROC 0.938 and F1 0.898. A 36,446-skill wild audit further shows that trace-attributed evidence surfaces actionable reuse review queues beyond repository-level baselines.

</details>


### [272] [Abstract Event Causal Rules: Induction and Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05205)
*Ziwei Zheng,Peiqiong Chen,Bang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出抽象事件因果规则（AECR），将具体的因果实例对泛化为关系级的抽象因果逻辑，并通过多智能体具体到抽象因果归纳（CACI）系统与相似性约束聚类构建知识库。同时设计AR-GCAE模型，将AECR注入因果图事件预测任务，显著提升了事件因果推理的泛化能力，尤其在稀有和未见事件上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实例级因果对在低频长尾和未见事件组合上存在严重的泛化缺陷，无法满足事件中心智能分析系统在风险预警、决策支持和叙事理解中的实际需求。因此需要一种能够将具体因果知识抽象化、提升泛化能力的新范式。

Method: (1) 提出关系级因果抽象范式——抽象事件因果规则（AECR），将具体因果对转化为保留内在因果关系的泛化抽象逻辑；(2) 设计多智能体具体到抽象因果归纳（CACI）系统，结合相似性约束聚类，从噪声原始因果数据中提炼可信赖的AECR，并构建两个完整的AECR知识库；(3) 提出抽象规则引导的因果注意力编码器（AR-GCAE），通过规则引导的注意力层和门控表示融合机制，将检索到的AECR注入因果图事件预测（CGEP）基准任务中。

Result: 定量实验表明，引入AECR显著增强了事件因果推理的泛化能力，并在事件预测任务上带来一致的性能提升，其中稀有和未见事件样本上的增益最为显著。

Conclusion: 抽象事件因果规则（AECR）作为一种关系级因果抽象范式，有效缓解了实例级因果知识的泛化瓶颈。通过CACI系统构建的AECR知识库与AR-GCAE模型的协同，验证了抽象因果知识在提升事件预测及因果推理鲁棒性方面的实用价值，尤其对长尾和未见场景具有重要推动作用。

Abstract: Event-centric intelligent analytical systems heavily depend on explicit causal event knowledge for risk early warning, decision-making support and narrative comprehension. Nevertheless, existing instance-level causal pairs suffer severe generalization deficits on low-frequency long-tail and unseen event combinations. To address this limitation, this work proposes Abstract Event Causal Rule (AECR), a novel relation-level causal abstraction paradigm that transforms concrete cause-effect pairs into generalized abstract causal logic while retaining their intrinsic causal relationships. We design a multi-agent Concrete-to-Abstract Causal Induction (CACI) system coupled with similarity-constrained clustering to distill trustworthy AECRs from noisy raw causal data, based on which two complete AECR knowledge bases are built. To validate the practical utility of abstract causal knowledge, we propose an Abstract Rule-Guided Causal Attention Encoder (AR-GCAE), which injects the retrieved AECRs into the causality Graph Event Prediction (CGEP) benchmark task via rule-guided attention layers and gated representation fusion. Quantitative experimental results reveal that applying AECRs substantially strengthens the generalization capacity of event causal reasoning and brings consistent performance improvements to event prediction, with the most prominent gains observed on rare and unseen event samples.

</details>
